iḷaṅkumaraṉār
tamiḻvaḷam
27
1. kaḻaka āṭciyar va.cu.varalāṟu

āciriyar :
mutu muṉaivar irā.iḷaṅkumaraṉ
vaḷavaṉ patippakam
ceṉṉai - 600 017.


nūṟ kuṟippu
nūṟpeyar	:	iḷaṅkumaraṉār - tamiḻvaḷam 27
āciriyar	:	irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
patippāḷar	:	kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ
patippu	:	2013
tāḷ	:	16 ki veḷḷaittāḷ
aḷavu	:	1/8 temmi
eḻuttu	:	11 puḷḷi
pakkam	:	16 + 368 = 384

vilai	:	urupā. 240/-

veḷiyīṭu
vaḷavaṉ patippakam
eṇ : 2 ciṅkāra vēlar teru,
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉai - 600 017.
tolaipēci : 24339030



patippurai
ilakkiya ilakkaṇac cemmal mutumuṉaivar ayyā iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷ eḻupattaintu āṇṭu niṟaivu eytiyataip pōṟṟum vakaiyil tamiḻ iḷaiyarkkeṉṟu avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷaiyellām cērttu eḻupattaintu nūlkaḷ kaṭanta 2005 - ām āṇṭu tiruccit tirunakaril veḷiyiṭṭu em patippup paṇikkup perumai cērttuk koṇṭōm. 
ipperuntamiḻ aṟiñar eḻutiya paṭaippukaḷai 20 tokutikaḷāka (1 mutal 20 varai) avariṉ 81-ām āṇṭu niṟaivaiyoṭṭi kaṭanta 2009 ām āṇṭil veḷiyiṭṭup perumai cērttōm. perumaikkup perumai cērkkum vakaiyil avar tam aṟivu viḷaiccalai 21 mutal 40 varai 
20 tokutikaḷai  83-ām āṇṭu (2012) niṟaivaiyoṭṭi tamiḻulakam payaṉkoṇṭu ceḻikkum vakaiyil veḷiyiṭukiṟōm. avar tam aruntamiḻp paṇiyai vaṇaṅki makiḻkiṟōm.
ipperumakaṉ vāḻum kālattilēyē ivar eḻutiya eḻuttukkaḷ, pēciya pēccukkaḷ aṉaittaiyum tokuttu veḷiyiṭuvatu eṉpatu tamiḻnūl patippu varalāṟṟil pōṟṟi makiḻvataṟkāṉa,  aṉaivarum piṉpaṟṟuvataṟkāṉa arumperum tamiḻp paṇiyākum.
ivar tannalam karutāmal tamiḻ nalam kāttu varupavar.  tammaimuṉṉiṟuttātu  tamiḻai muṉṉiṟuttum peruntamiḻaṟiñar.  tamakkeṉa vāḻātu tamiḻkkeṉa vāḻpavar. orunāḷiṉ muḻup
poḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻum tamiḻp pōrāḷi. caṅkac cāṉṟōr varicaiyil vaittu eṇṇattakka aruntamiḻaṟiñar. tamiḻ ilakkaṇa - ilakkiya marapaik kāttu varum marapu vaḻi aṟiñar. āravāram mikunta iṉṟaiya cūḻnilaiyil paṭāṭōpam iṉṟiyum, viḷamparap pōlimai iṉṟiyum,   tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ āḻa akalaṅkaḷai akaḻntu kāṭṭum toltamiḻaṟiñar. moḻinūl katiravaṉ pāvāṇariṉ vērccol āyvil avar kāṭṭiya vaḻiyil tam tamiḻāyvait  toṭarpavar. 
tamiḻāyvuk kaḷattil  tam āyvuppayaṇattait toṭaṅkum tamiḻ āyvāḷarkaḷukkum, tamiḻ uṇarvāḷarkaḷukkum, māṇavac celvaṅkaḷukkum taṅkat taṭṭil vaittup poṟ
kuviyalākat  tantuḷḷōm. em tamiḻ nūl patippup payaṇattil,  tamiḻtteṉṟal tiru.vi.ka.vaiyum, taṉittamiḻiyakkat tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷaiyum, moḻinūl katiravaṉ pāvāṇaraiyum, tamiḻ moḻikkāvalar ilakkuvaṉāraiyum vaḻikāṭṭiyākak koṇṭu tamiḻnūl parappiṉ ellaiyaik kaṇṭu kāṭṭum caṅkattamiḻc cāṉṟōrāka viḷaṅkum aiyā iḷaṅkumaraṉār  vāḻum kālattilēyē avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭuvatai yām peṟṟa pēṟākak karutukiṟōm. tamiḻar illamtōṟum irukkattakka ivvaruntamiḻc celvaṅkaḷait tamiḻ iḷam talaimuṟaikku vaippākak koṭuttuḷḷōm. 
 calukai pōṉāl pōkaṭṭum - eṉ 
	aluval pōṉāl pōkaṭṭum 
	talaimuṟai orukōṭi kaṇṭa - eṉ
	tamiḻ viṭutalai ākaṭṭum.
pāvēntar varikaḷai niṉaivil koḷvōm.
- patippāḷar.




peṟum pēṟu
vaḷam eṅkē irukkiṟatu?
vaḷam eṅkēyum irukkiṟatu! aḵtu illāta iṭamē illai! 
vaḷam eṅkum iruppatai eppaṭi aṟiyalām?
vaḷattait tēṭum uḷam iruntāl, vaḷam eṅkēyum iruppatai aṟiyalām?
vaḷattiṉ aḷavu eṉṉa?
vaḷattiṉ aḷavum uḷattiṉ aḷavēyām!
uḷattil vaḷam illaiyāṉāl, uḷḷa vaḷamum uṭaiyavaṉukkum utavātu ūrukkum ulakukkum utavātu!
vaḷattait tarum uḷattai oruvar vāykkap peṟṟāl pōtum!
avvaḷam kuṉṟiyaḷavāṉāl eṉṉa?
     kuṉṟattaṉaiya vaḷamāṉāl eṉṉa?
     tiṉai aḷavāṉāl eṉṉa?
     paṉai aḷavāṉāl eṉṉa?
     vaḷam vaḷamē!
tēṉī vaḷam? tiṉaittaṉai aḷavup pūvil, tēṉī koḷḷum tēṉ evvaḷavu irukkum?
kōttumpi koṇarum tēṉ vaḷam evvaḷavu irukkum?
kōttumpi koṇarvatē vaḷam, tēṉī koṇarvatu vaḷamillai eṉa evarāvatu kūṟuvārā?
vaḷam vaḷamē! avaravar uḻaippu, aṟivuttiṟam, uṇmaiyaṟivu, paṭṭaṟivu, kāla-iṭa-cūḻal koṭai- ākiyavaṟṟaip poṟuttatu atu.
poruḷ maṭṭumā vaḷam?
pulamai vaḷamillaiyā?
ūkkutal utavutal uṟutuṇaiyātal oṉṟutal āyavai - vaḷamillaiyā?
ellām vaḷamē! mallal vaḷaṉē eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. (uri.7) uṭalālum vaḷam; uḻaippālum vaḷam; uṇarvālum vaḷam; uraiyālum vaḷam; vaḷam paṭa vāḻa vēṇṭātār evar? vaḷam peṟa vēṇṭātār evar?
vaḷantalai mayaṅkiya vañci muṟṟam
eṉpatu cilampu! vaḷattiṉ aḷavu, aḷantu kaṭai aṟiyāvaḷam eṉpatu (25:33-34)
nāṭeṉpa nāṭā vaḷattaṉa
eṉpatu nāṭṭiṉ ilakkaṇam! eḷimai, iṉimai, iṟaimai, niṟaimai eṉa makkaḷ vāḻnta nāḷ ilakkaṇam! iṉṟō,
nāṉila vaḷaṅkaḷaiyum nāṭā nāṭillai!
enta nāṭu ēṟṟumati ceyyavillai?
enta nāṭu iṟakkumati ceyyavillai?
pālaivaḷattai nāṭiccōlai niṟṟal veḷippaṭai eṉṟāl, colvāṉēṉ?
nāṭāvaḷattil kuppaikkīrai cērātā? kuṟuntūṟu cērātā?
vaḷattai vaḻaṅkutal tāṉ piṟavik kaṭaṉē yaṉṟi aḷaviṭum poṟuppu vaḻaṅkuvārkku illai! peṟuvār poṟuppu. āṉāl,
vaḷam peṟṟup peṟṟup peruki vāḻntavar, avvaḷattait tamakku vaḻaṅkiya maṇṇukku maṟittu vaḻaṅkip peṟṟa kaṭaṉait tīrkkāmal pōṉāl kaṭaṉkārarākavē pōvatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, piṟavi aṭaiyāḷamē illāmalum pōyviṭuvār!
peṟṟavar vaḻiyākak karuvilē peṟṟa tiru eṉṉa! kaṟṟa kalviyāl peṟṟa vaḷam eṉṉa! paṭṭaṟivāl - āḻmaṉa āyvāl - tēṭik koṇṭa vaḷaṅkaḷ eṉṉa eṉṉa! piñcup paruva mutal perum pirivu varai peṟṟavaṟṟai, annilaipparuvattarkkup paruvamaḻai pōlap palappala vakaiyālum vaḻaṅkutal vāḻvār vāḻvuc cīrmaiyātal vēṇṭum!
purivu terinta nāḷ mutal pulamai perukivarum aḷavukkut takat-takat tolkāppiyar āṇai vaḻi,
ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
         āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam
eṉṉum nālneṟik kaṭaṉkaḷaiyum niṟaivēṟṟal vēṇṭum! kūṟṟaiyum āṭal koḷḷum koḷkai ituveṉa uṇarvār ōyār, oḻiyār; cāyār; carintum pōkār!
eṉ iḷantaip paruvamē, āciriyap paruvamākiviṭṭatu.  akkālac cūḻal atu.
piṟantanāḷ : 30.01.1930
āciriyap paṇi ēṟṟa nāḷ: 08.04.1946
kāl caṭṭai pōṭum māṇavapparuvattil vēṭṭi kaṭṭum āciriyaṉāki viṭṭēṉ! mūṉṟāṇṭu āciriyap paṇi ceytāl pōtum! pulavar tērvait taṉiyāka eḻutalām eṉṉum vāyppu! avvāyppaic cikkeṉap paṟṟiyamaiyāl 1949, 1950, 1951 ākiya mūvāṇṭukaḷil toṭarntu nuḻaivut tērvu, pulavar iṭainilait tērvu, pulavar niṟai nilait tērvu eṉac ciṟappuṟa veṟṟi vāyppu eytiyatu.  uyarpaḷḷi, mēlpaḷḷi eṉpavaṟṟilum palkalaik kaḻakat tamiḻiyal āyvuk kaḷattilumāka ēṟattāḻa 43 āṇṭukaḷ tamiḻvāḻvāka vāḻa vāyttatu.  tamiḻttoṇṭum, tamiḻneṟit toṇṭum eṉṟum viṭutal aṟiyā viruppoṭu ceytu koṇṭiruttalē iyaṟkaiyōṭu iyainta vāḻvāki viṭṭatu!
pulamait tērvukkuriya pāṭattaḷavilō, nūlkaḷiṉ aḷavilō eṉ kalvi niṉṟatu illai! tolkāppiyam mutal iṟṟaināḷ nūlkaḷ varai iyaṉṟa aḷavāl tamiḻvaḷam peṟutalai nōṉpākak koṇṭu kaṟkum pēṟupeṟṟēṉ.  pērāciriyar ci.ilakkuvaṉār, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar, nāvalar ca.cō.pāratiyār, varalāṟṟuc cemmal aracamāṇikkaṉār, paintamiḻp pāvalar m.ki.guªjhkdh®, uraivēntar auvai cu.turaicāmiyār, mūtaṟiñar cemmal va,cupa.māṇikkaṉār, tāmaraic celvar va,cuppaiyaṉār, īrōṭu vēlā, kuḻittalai mī.cu. iḷamuruku poṟcelvi, patippuc cemmal meyyappaṉār, pārati cō.cāminātaṉār, tamiḻppōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉār, tamiḻ mīṭpukkut talainiṉṟa āṉārūṉā, pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷaiyar kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār, malaiyaka māriyappaṉār iṉṉar nerukkat toṭarpukaḷ vāyttaṉa.
iḷantaip paruvattilēyē pāvēntar, ōki cuttāṉanta pāratiyār, kavimaṇiyār, kavirāca paṇṭita cekavīra
pāṇṭiyaṉār, aṟiñar mu.va. āyōr ariya kāṭciyum ciṉmuṟaic cantippukaḷum eṉṉuḷ pacumai nalkiṉa. 
tamiḻp poḻivukaḷum, āyvuk kaṭṭuraikaḷum, kuṭumpac caṭaṅkukaḷum eṉ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṇikaḷ āyiṉa!
em kuṭumpak kaṭamaikaḷ em paṇiyaiyō toṇṭaiyō kavarntu koḷḷā vakaiyil iṉiya tuṇaiyum makkaḷum amaintaṉar.
ataṉāl, kālamellām kaṟkavum kaṟpikkavum nūl
paṭaikkavum vāytta vāyppup peritāyiṟṟu.
aṉpum aṟivum eṉṉum nūl toṭaṅkic centamiḻc coṟporuḷ kaḷañciyam eṉat toṭarntu ēṟattāḻa 400 nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟavum veḷiyiṭavum vāyttaṉa.
kuḻantai nūlkaḷ, nūlaka nūlkaḷ, āyvu nūlkaḷ, akarātikaḷ, urainūlkaḷ, patippu nūlkaḷ, pā nūlkaḷ, kāppiyaṅkaḷ, kataikaḷ, kaṭṭuraikaḷ, tokuppukaḷ ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ eṉap palappala tiṟattaṉavāyp pāl vāyp paruvattar mutal palkalaik kaḻaka āyvar varaikkum payaṉ koḷḷum vakaiyil avai amaintaṉa.
etai eṭuttālum pāṭalākkal orukālam; kataiyum nāṭakamum ākkal orukālam; patippup paṇiyē oru kālam; urai
kāṇalē oru kālam; paṭippaṭiyē collāyvē vāḻveṉa ākiviṭṭa kālam eṉa amaintaṉa.
ik koṭaikaḷ vaḻaṅkiyavai pārati patippakam, caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, maṇi patippakam, murukaṉ patippakam, vēlā patippakam, maṇivācakar patippakam, vēmaṉ patippakam, mūvēntar patippakam, pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷai, cākittiya akātemi, ulakat tamiḻ ārāycci niṟuvaṉam, tamiḻmaṇ patippakam,  amiḻtam patippakam, vaḷavaṉ patippakam, māṇavar patippakam, amiḻtamaṇi patippakam, tiruvaḷḷuvar tavaccālai aṟakkaṭṭaḷai veḷiyīṭukaḷ eṉa virintaṉa.
nūlurup peṟṟu accaka vaḻiyum patippaka vaḻiyum kāṇappeṟāmalum etirpārā maṟaivukaḷālum ēṟattāḻac ciṟitum peritumāy oḻintavai irupatukku mēl āyiṉa.
centamiḻ, centamiḻc celvi, tamiḻppāvai, kuṟaḷ
neṟi, kuṟaḷiyam, malarkaḷ, āyvaraṅkaṅkaḷ eṉak kaṭṭuraikaḷ perukiṉa.  accukku varātavaiyum ākiṉa!  ivai eṉṟum orumukamāyk kiṭaikka vāyppillai.
em nūlkaḷ emmiṭamē muppatukkumēl illāmal maṟaintaṉa.  eḻutiyaṉa ellāvaṟṟaiyum orumukamākap peṟap
palar avāviṉār. tavattiru āttumāṉanta aṭikaḷār (āruyirkku aṉpar) atil talaippaṭṭu niṉṟār.  āyar āṇṭakai cūcaimāṇikkaṉār cuṭarēṟṟiṉār. pāratitācaṉ palkalaikkaḻakam matippuṟu mutumuṉaivar paṭṭam vaḻaṅkiyatu.  ataṟku makiḻvuṟṟa vakaiyāl pāvāṇar tamiḻiyakkam mutalām amaippukaḷum, muṉaivar tirumāṟaṉār mutalām ārvalarkaḷum viḻā oṉṟu eṭuttaṉar.  avviḻāt talaimaiyait tiruttaku tuṇaivēntar poṉṉavaikkō ēṟṟār.  tavattiru āttumāṉanta aṭikaḷār vāḻtturaittār.  poṟiñar tamiḻaṟiñar pālakaṅkātaraṉār eṉ nūlkaḷ cilavaṟṟai oḷippaṭiyākki uyaraṭṭaik kaṭṭil vaḻaṅki avaṟṟai muḻutuṟap peṟa mīḷacciṭutal vēṇṭum eṉṟār.  tamiḻākkap patippē vāḻvāka oṉṟiya iḷavaḻakaṉār muḻutuṟa acciṭa oppiṉār.  aṭikaḷār veḷiyīṭṭup poṟuppu ēṟpatākak kūṟiṉār. kiṭaikkum nūlkaḷ tavirttup piṟavaṟṟaiyellām acciṭum tiṭṭam koṇṭu ēṟattāḻa irupatu, irupatu tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭuvatu eṉa uṟutippaṭuttap paṭṭatu.  avvakaiyil vāyppatē, iḷaṅkumaraṉār tamiḻvaḷam eṉṉum it tokai nūlkaḷām! it tokaiyil viṭupaṭum nūlkaḷ kaṭṭuraikaḷ, pāṭalkaḷ palapala;  tokukka vāykkā vakaiyil kiṭṭāvakaiyil maṟappu vakaiyil viṭupāṭuṭaiyavai uḷa.  eṉiṉum toṇṇuṟu viḻukkāṭēṉum oru mukamākap peṟum pēṟu vāykkiṉṟatu eṉpatu makiḻvūṭṭuvatām!
makkaḷ vāḻvu vaḷarnilaiyatu! nūlkaḷum vaḷar nilaiyavai! ātalāl oruvāḻvuk koṭai maṭṭumaṉṟu ītu; oruvāḻvu peṟat takka palparuvak koṭaiyumām eṉal cālum! itaṉ aṭippaṭaiyām ettēṭalilum peritum tōyāmal tamiḻvaḷat tēṭalilēyē kālamellām cōmpalaic cuṭṭerittu, iṭukkaṇukku iṭukkaṇ ākki vāḻnta vāḻvuk koṭaiyām itu!
ivvaḷattai muḻutuṟa eṇṇiṉ oṉṟu uṟutiyāka veḷippaṭum.  nalavāḻvu nalvāḻvu karutāta - iyaṟkai iṟaimaip potumai karutāta - eppaṭaippum empaṭaippil irā eṉpatēyām.  ivaṟṟaip payilvār peṟum pēṟum atuvāka amaiyiṉ appēṟē yām peṟum pēṟām.
ivvaḷam veḷivara virumpiṉōr uḻaittōr utaviṉōr āya aṉaivarkkum naṉṟik kaṭappāṭuṭaiyēṉ! vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
nellukku umiyuṇṭu
nīrukku nuraiyuṇṭu
pullitaḻ pūvukkum uṇṭu
kuṟaikaḷaintu niṟai peytu
kaṟṟal, kaṟpōr kaṭaṉeṉal muntaiyar muṟai
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ,
irā.iḷaṅkumaraṉ

kaḻaka āṭciyar va.cu.varalāṟu


maturai kāmarācar palkalaikkaḻakam
tuṇaivēntar, ṭākṭar. va.cupa.māṇikkam avarkaḷ aṉpuṭaṉ aḷitta


aṇinturai

tāmaraic celvar tamiḻp perumakaṉ cuppaiyā piḷḷai varalāṟu taṉiyoruvar varalāṟaṉṟu, nūṟṟitaḻt tāmarai pōlat tamiḻmoḻik kāppaiyum vaḷarcciyaiyum perumaiyaiyum pulamaiyaiyum taṉṉuṭkoṇṭa māperu varalāṟākum. taṉit tamiḻiyakkam, intiyetirppu iyakkam, tamiḻicaik kiḷarcci, tamiḻ vaḻipāṭu, tamiḻāṭci, tūya tamiḻitaḻ, accakam, nūlnilaiyam, tamiḻk kalaikaḷ, pulavar viḻā, pulavaraip pōṟṟal eṉpaṉavellām ivar varalāṟṟoṭu toṭarpuṭaiyavai.
periyavar va. cuppaiyā piḷḷai tamiḻvaḷarccikkut tāmē kaṇṭa patippu neṟikaḷum nūṉmuṟaikaḷum talaippukaḷum viyakkattakkavai. coṟpoḻivu eṉṟa nūl vakaiyil caṅka mutal tamiḻilakkiyam ellām kālattiṟkēṟṟa kaivaṭivu peṟṟaṉa, nūṟṟukku mēlāṉa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ matinuṭpattaiyum eḻuttu vaṉmaiyaiyum tiruvuruvaṅkaḷaiyum coṟpoḻivu eṉṉum ivvurai naṭaic ciṟṟilakkiyam potintu vaittiruk kiṉṟatu. etirkāla āyñarkku ivai toṭumaṇaṟ kēṇiyākum. caṅka ilakkiyap pēḻai eṉpatu tamiḻukkut taṅka illap pēḻai eṉpataṉai varalāṟu collum. kuḻaviyar, ciṟār, iḷaiñar, mutiyōr, peṇṭir, ayalōr, māṟṟu moḻiyiṉar eṉa aṉaitiṟattiṉarkkum pakkuva nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭa pār pukaḻ tamiḻttoṇṭu tiruvaraṅkaṉār tōṟṟiya va. cuppaiyā piḷḷai nilaināṭṭiya kaḻakattiṟkē uriyatu.
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ nūlnilaiyam eṉpatu vilaimatikka muṭiyāta aruṅkalai māḷikaiyākum. tāmaraiccelvar tam muyaṟciyāl tamiḻaṉṉaikkuk kaṭṭiya tamiḻaṇi nilaiyam eṉṟu ulakaṟiñar pārāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. evvaḷavu iḷaiya aṟivuṭai nampikaḷ innilaiyattiṟku nāḷtoṟum ceṉṟu iruntu kuṟipput tokuttu āyvai vitaikkiṉṟaṉar. iḵtu aṟivāṟṟuppaṭaiyāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
etirmaṟaiyākap pārttāṟṟāṉ meyt toṇṭukaḷiṉ ēṟṟaṅkaḷ pulappaṭum. caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam eṉa ōr amaippu illātiruntāl, kaḻakap pāṭanūlkaḷ illāti runtāl, caṅkavilakkiyaṅkaṭkum, kāppiyaṅkaṭkum, tolkāppiya mutalāṉa ilakkaṇaṅkaṭkum ciṟṟilakkiyaṅkaṭkum, uraip patippukkaḷum, mēluraip patippukkaḷum, aṭakkap patippuk kaḷum, urainaṭaip patippukkaḷum illātiruntāl, mu.va.viṉ teḷivurai, tirukkuṟaḷ nāṭ kuṟippu, tiruvaḷḷuvar malar mutalāṉa malarkaḷ, moḻi peyarppukaḷ, pulavar varalāṟukaḷ, aṟiviyaṟ kottukkaḷ, vaṭamoḻi tamiḻakara varicaikaḷ, centamiḻccelvi illātiruntāl, malaikaḷiṟ ciṟanta tamiḻmalaiyāṉa maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷiṉ kaṭalaṉaiya nūlnilaiyam illātiruntāl, ittuṇaikkum viṉaimutalvarāṉa tiruvaraṅkaṉāriṉ iḷavalāṉa tāmaraic celvariṉ teyva vuṇarvum, kuṟikkōḷ vīramum, nīṭiya āyuḷum illātiruntāl, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭut tamiḻaṉṉai oru kuḻantai tūkkic cellum melluruvamāka iruntiruppapāḷ. tāmaraip piḷḷaiyum kaḻakamum tōṉṟi vaḷarntu āṟṟiya uḻavut toṇṭiṉāl nam tamiḻaṉṉai talaipalavum, kaikāl plavum peṟṟu nāṭi narampellām nūlmuṟukkēṟik kuruti aṟivoḷi peruki, ticai nāṉkum ōṭi, aintu kōṭi makkaḷum pattukkōṭit tōḷil cemmāntu tūkkumaḷavu uyirvaḷam taḻaittirukkiṉṟāḷ. ippukaḻcci perumikaiyaṉṟu.
toṇṭāṟpaḻutta tamiḻk kiḻavar va. cuppaiyāpiḷḷai varalāṟṟiṉait toyyā naṭaiyil murukupaṭa eḻutiyuḷḷār pulavar iḷaṅkumaraṉār. pakutit talaippukaḷ ilakkiya maṇaṅ kamaḻkiṉṟaṉa. kāla muṟaiyākac ceytip puṉaiviṉṟiyum naṭaippuṉaivōṭum iv varalāṟu amaintuḷḷatu. tāmaraic celvariṉ vāḻvu virivai aṟintār innūlai ōr aṭakkap patippākavē karutuvār. eṉiṉum im mutaṟ patippu putiya ceytikaḷ pala potinta mūlappatippākum.
iv varalāṟṟaip paṭippār, tamiḻaip paṭippār, tamiḻt toṇṭu ceyvār, toṇṭāḷarkaḷai matippār, tamiḻp piṟappai maṉaṅ koḷvār. tamiḻt toṇṭiṉāl tamakkum oru varalāṟu varumpaṭi tamiḻvāḻvu naṭattuvār. ituvē tāmaraic celvar tamiḻarpāl etirpārkkum taṉṉalam.


FOREWORD
Dr. K.R.SRINIVASA IYENGAR
Retd. Vice-Chancellor
Andhra University.
Former Vice - President
Sathithya Akademi, Delhi.
It is with pleasure that I Write this Foreword to Pulavar 
R. Ilankumaran’s detailed account of the life-work and times of Tiru Va Subbiah Pillau, whose name has become almost synonymous with the Personality, drive and achievements of the Tirunelveli South India Saiva Siddhanta Book- Publishing Kazhagam and its several affiliated instittuions. I have been a distant admirer of Tiru Va. Su for many years, and since my boyhood years in Tirunelveli. I have watched the Saiva Siddhanta Book-Publishing Society grow from its early auspicious beginings to its prsent impressive strength and healthy wide-ranging influence. I therefore welcome this opportunity to offer my own tribute to the Man and the Kaxhagam he has fostered with such care and clear sense of direction.
Dr. Johnson’s words “slow rises worth by poverty depressed” had a close application to the first phase of Tiru Va Su’s life and career, Tlhe fourth son of Vairamuthu Pillai and Sundarathammayar of Palayamkottai, Subbiah losthis father when barely three, and two of his brothers and his only sister before he was twenty-five. On his elder brother, Tiruvaranganar, senior by seven years, fell the responsibility of sustaining the family throught its difficult years. He found employment in a commercial establishment in Colombo in 1907, and during the next ten years, his monthly remittance were the mainstay of the family. In the meantime, Subbiah passed his S.S.L.C. in 1916 from St. Xavier’s High School, Palayamkottai, and proceeded to Colombo to continue his studies. But not long after, illness made him return to Palayamkottai, however, he duly passed the London Matric in 1917 as a private candidate. Failing to secure admissin to the Madras Medical College in 1918, Va Su groped about for a while, till he presently found his great mission in life as salesman of the nectar of Tamil letters.
During his sojourn inColombo, Va Su’s brother, Tiru varanganar, made the acquaintance of the notred Tamil scholar and writer, Maraimalai Adigal, arranged for the savant’s memorable first visit to Colombo in 1914, and stimulated the sale of his books. It was during his second visit in 1917 that Va Su met the Adigal andlaid the foundation of his Boswellian relationship to the Master. That year also saw the establishment by Tiruvaranganar of Tiru - Sankar Company, a bookshop in Colimbo for the sale of the works of Maraimalai Adigal and other scholars. Tiruvaranganar, however, Soon realised that his real work lay inTamil Nadu, and hence moved to Coral Merchant Street, Madras, to organise the bookshop Tiru-Sankar Company, while asking Va Su to open in the family home at Palayamkottai a branch of the firm. Tiruvaranganar and Va Su firmly believed that the sale of good books, being verily the commerce of idean and knowledge and values., blessed the givers and takers alkie; no surfeit in the receiving, and no loss in the giving!
Tlhe next decisinve turn was the incorporation by the brothers, on 21 September 1920, of the Tirunelveli South India Saiva Siddhanta Book-publishing Kazhagam. The brother received  much encouragement from Tiru M. Diraviyam Pillai and Tiru T.S. Visvanatha Pillai, and the Kazhagam commenced its career with and authorised capital of Rs. 50,000, made up of 5000 shares, each of Rs. 10. The capital was to be increased in 1952 to Rs. 1 lakh, and in 1963 to Rs. 2 lakhs. With the opening of the Madras Branch of the Kazhagam in February 1921, with Va Su as Manager on Rs. 35 per month (Tiruvaranganar shifting to the Tirunelveli Head Office), the Tirunelveli - Madras axis startedfunctioning with quiet efficiency and a change of missinary zeal.
The new publishing firm was active from the very beginning. The bookshop Tiru - Sankar Company. and the Saiva Siddhanta Book-publishing Corporation teamed together and merged, and in 1923 a Tamil monthly literary journal, ‘Senthamil Selvi’, waslaunched as an auciliary force pointing towards the same end. In book-publishing, the new firm aimed at the diffusionof religious (especially Saiva), ethical, and worthy secular literature, classical as well as modern, and worthy secular literature, classicalas well as modern, and also at promoting a two-way Suez Canal-like traffic in letters:: Tamil into other languages,and Singnificant literature in other languages (notably English) into Tamil. There was to the a stress also on Scholarship, exegesis research, literary criticism and interpretaton. The first book to be issued under the Kazhagam imprint was, appropriately enough, Tiruvadi Pugazhamalai, and while the first year saw the publication of five books, the 2nd saw seven, the 3rdthriteen, the 4th twenty, and so on. Forty years after, In 1961, the 1008th book came out, and today  the total in 1750 or more, averaging 25 per year, or one bookfor every 13 days during the entire 60-year period of the Kazhagam’s existence.
Tiruvaranganar at Tirunelveli and Va Su at Madrasconstituted between them a resourceful and energetic publishing duumvirate for about 25 years, from 1920 to 1944. Good book-publishing is a taxing and trcky vocation that calls for a rare union of secular and spiritual qualities. A book has a soul as well as a body, aham and not alone puram it calls for the mobilisation of several instruments of production. Buildings, printing facilities, finance, editorial expertise,talent for marketing. pleasing and effective public relations, and above all a flair for the location of writing talent and its mobilisation :: all are needed. AndTiruvaranganar and Va. Su. were able to assemble a dedicated group of writers andeditors, and publish a body of literature (mainly in Tamil), both impressive in bulk and marked by a varegated richness. Tiruvaranganar’s marriage to Maraimalai Adigal’s daughter, Nilambikai, in 1927, and Va Su’s to Mangayarkarasi (daughterof Tiru Aundiappa Pillai of Palayamkottai), in 1935 brought a humanising influence to the brothers’ lives; and Tiruvaranganar in Tirunelvaly and Va Su in Madras worked in perfect co-ordination devoted to the claims of the hearth and equally to the exaction demands of book-publishing as well as advancing the other aims of the Kazhagam.
The untimely death of Tiruvaranganar, th Father of the Kazhagam, in 1944, followed by that of Nilambikai Ammal next year, was a double blow under which the publishing firm could not but reel for a time. It now became necessary for Va Su’s wife, Mangayakarasi, to shift to Palayamkottai with her two daughters, so as to be able to look after Tiruvaranganar;s children and the aged mother-in-law., During the next eight years, Va Su gallan-tly contined at Madras, cookming his own meals, and living a life of simplicity, austerity and infallible regularity. And he laboured with redoubled dedication and saw that the progress of the Kazhagam didn’t suffer,whether in Madras or at Tirunelveli, He bought the Janasakthi Press for Rs.20,000, re named it Appar Achakam, and had it opened by Omandur Ramaswami Reddiar in 1948. This gave a push to the Kazhagam’s book-publishing activities and helped it to build an opulently diversified body of publications in a variety of formats;; mini, medium and large, paperback, calico, rexine,popular, and gold-lettered deluxe! learned commentaries on Tolappiyam, and other works of grammar;; the whole ensemble of the Sangam Classics;; the minor but no less significant literary forms (ula, kalambakam, thoothu, Kuravanji, andadi, pillaithamizh etc.) ;; the celebrated Tirukkural in a diversity of editons, one of them (with Mu Varadarjanar’s paraphrase) achieving a sale of a million;; the classics of religions divination and ecastasy like Tirumandiram, Tevaram, Tiruvasagam and Tiruppugazh;; philosophical classics like Meikandar’s;; the Sangam Classics in prose or in summary reecitals;; collections of speeches and conference papers;; historical and scientific works;; secular literature like fiction, essays and drama; children’s literature; the works of Maraimalai Adigal; translations; research monographs in Tamil and in English; biographies, dictionaries..........well, God’s plenty, in fact. The Kazhagam’s list is a composite and comprehensive library by itself. No wonder it has been remarked that the Kazhagam, as an instittution, is older than the Annamalai,and Madurai KamarajUniversities; and its record of scholoarly publications in Tamil is more outstanding than that of the University of Mazdras.
In September 1950, the death of Maraimalai Adigal left a big void in theTamil world. By his last will and testament, he hadbequeathed his very considerable privare library of rare Tamil books and other scholarly works to the Kazhagam, and this priceless collection of about 4000 books was to become the necleusof the Maraimalai Adigal Memorial Library, which was duly opened by Tiru. M. Bhaktavatsalam on 24th August 1958, in a historic building in Linghi Chetti Street, The building has seminal associations with Ramalinga Vallalar, who is said to have delivered his rirst impromptu discourse there to and astonished assembly. The Library began with the 4000 of the Maraimalai Adigal collection and anothe 5000 in the Kazhagam’s garner. Since then there have been steady accessions, includiug whole libraries like that of the historian,the late V. Rangachari; and today the library has about 35,000 books and rare manuscripts. Although located in a crowded part of the city, the Library is the haunt of Tamil scholars old and yound; and, for the committed, it is what anideal Library shouldbe, a Houseof Perpetual Enlightenment. I may be added that Maraimalai Adigal’s ownhouse in Pallavaramhas also been turned by the Kazhagaminto a memorial Kalai Manram, and is serving the cause of arts and letters during the last two decades.
What becomes clear when lone reads Pulavar Ilan kumaran’s account of the life of Tiru.Va. Subbiah Pilai is that the biography merges with the history of a dedicated family, as also with that of Kazhagamand its collateral institutions, and even with that of the Pure Tamil movement and the Tamil literary renaissance witnessed during the last sixty years. Tiruvaranganar and Va Su were closely linkedwith Maraimalai Adigal as disciples and heirs to his ministry in the service of Tamil. If Tiruvaranganar became the Adigal’s son-in-law also, on Va Su fell the responsibility of cherishing and fostering the Maraimalai tradiontgs of integrity and high seriousness in Tamil scholarship. After performing the marriage of Tiruvaranganar’s daughters, Mayilamami and Muthammal, Va Su thought of the marriageof his own daughters Vadivazhagiar and Vairamaniyar. The former was married in 1956 to Kalyansundaram, Va Su’s brother-in-law’s elder son. The younger son, Mutukumaraswamy married Vairamani in 1959. And in yound Muthukumaraswamy - young but fully trained in Library Science-the Maraimalai Adigal Library has found a seasoned libratian,who has fosteredits growth in the right directions andmade it a veritable mecca for advanced Tamil studies and research.
Between 1920,the year of the Kazhagam’s foundation, and today over sixty years have passed, For the first 25 years, the Tiruvarangam-Va Su duumvirate gave the right leadership to the Kazhagam. After 1944, althoughg Va Suhad to take upon himself the responsibilities at Madrasa swell as at Tirunelveli, he had good collaborators, and found in his sons-in-law admirable executants. At Tirunelveli, the Sivagnana Munivar’s Library, the Thiruvalluvar Tamil College and the Kumaraguruparar Nursery School came up one after another. Branches of the Kazhagam have been established at Madurai, Coimbatore, Kumbakonam and Tiruchirappalli. Although never in exceptionally robust health (he had a period of acute illness at Jaffna in 1951), Va Su has both kept going the different institutions under his charge in a high pitch of efficiency and also constantly endeavoured to add new dimensins to their activities. He is a master builder, really:: he is a Kazhagam by himself, and always a power for good.
The incorporation of the Saiva Siddhanta Book-Publishing Foundation was an act of faith, a blow struck in favour of Tamil language, literature and culture. Like Maraimalai Adigal,the Kazhagam and Va Su have been advocation with evangelical fervour the ‘Pure Tami’ movement, it was Suryanarayena Sastri who first changed his Sanskritised name into the Tamil ‘Parithimar Kalaignar’ Likewise ‘Swami Vedachalam’ underwent a sea-change into ‘<araimalai Adigal’. In the pase there had doubtles been a priod of heavy and sometimes humourless Sanskritisation of Tamil  with its native, homely and beautiful usages. This corresponded to the Latinisation, in mediaveval times, of English (or Anglo-Saxon) forms. In the 19th century, however,there was a determined move in England to prefer always the Saxon to the more recondite or polysyllabic Latin word. But this too some times led to absurdities, and in course of time, after the swong between thesis and antithesis, the English language has been trying to arrive at a sensible, intelligible and lively synthesis, with  a broad baseof unity that nevertheless permits surface variations depending on the locality, occasion or speaker, In Tamil Nadu, again.,the earlier Sanskritisation and the ,ore recent Anglicisation have tended to obscure the native hue and force and beautyy of Tamil, and the determined effort to return to easy and natural Tamil usage is certainly to be welcomed. On the other hand, linguistic ‘purism’ - in any extremely narrow sense of the term - is no virtue, and in the modern world teeming with new ideas, siturations and technologies, can even hamper the natural growth of a language. In hisintroduction to my Literature and Authorship in India (1943), E.M<. Forster described himself as a ‘convinced impurist”, implying that linguistic purism should not be pursued at the expense of intelligibility and the dynamic of linguistic growth in a fast-changing world. While without question there should be broadly understood norms of spoken and written language, a single adamantiune- law cannot be made to apply to all speakers and users of a language, pursuing as they may diversoccupatons, and caughtin all kinds of situations.
The Kazhagam’s and Tiru Va Su’s meritorious efforst to safeguard the sovereign interests of Tamil and to resist aggression by otehr languages (Hindi, for example) areworthy of all praise, but I also feel that within a broad framework of unity and uniformity, a certain play of variation in individual and situational usage is inevitable, and will be a signof healthy growth, And great and ancient and uniquely rich as is the Tamil language, it cannot quite ignore the need to build bridgesof understanding with the other languages of India, the classical Sanskrit and the living regional languages.
It is worth mentioning also that the Kazhagam, under Va Su’s sleepless stewardship, has organised conferencesand Tamil Vizhas (festivals) to emplore the richesof Tamil leters and popularise the values and verities of the literature, are and cultureof the Tamil country. Thus there have been almost 20 conferences (at Tirunelveli, Madras and Madurai) on classics like pathuppattu, Nattrinai, Ettuthohai, Kalithohai, Purananooru, Theshorter epics and so on, and the lecturesgiven and the papers presented have been collected and published by the Kazhagam. As for the festivals, there was the first Tiruvalluvar Vizha at Pachayappa’s College in 1935, amemorable event. Othershave follwed, theannual Maraimalai Adigal festivals, periodic festivals for book-releases or in commemoration of particular poetes and literary celebrities, seminarson select themes, the festival in 1977 in connection with Senbthamil SElvi’s golden junbilee - these and other vixhas, carefully planned and organish\ed with every attention to detail by Va Su and his collegues, hav \e done their best ot keep the Flame of Tamil letters and culture alive and aloft.
Aside from his manifold activities directed towards the realisations of the far aims of the Kazhagam and its connected institutions, VA Suis knownin his own right as a writer, especially of books for children. He wrote for his children first, and his books have since become a legacy for all children, Besides, the kazhagam has sponsored the publication of about 300 books for children. For Va Su the past is his foundation, the present the arena of activity, and the future the threatre of his dreams and hopes and plans. He was one of the spirits behind the Tamil Protection SAngam launched in 1937, and he has outlined his 21-point programmed for the conservation and development of Tamil. With his uncanny flair for recognising and enlisting writing talent for engineering effectively the renaissance in Tamil letters, he has been able to build the Kazhagam’s massive and may- sided publishing list. He takes a midwife’s personal interestin almost every book that appears over the Kazhagam imprint, and he is said to attend to proof-reading even today.
And in sun, how shale we take the measure of the man VA Su or of his work spread over 60 years and in so many directions? Pulavar Illankumaran has gathered together a number of tributes, in prose and in verse, to VA Su by a galaxy of his contemporaries, admirers and friends. A dutiful son, brother, householder and patriarch of the family; a man of ceaselessindustrylabbouring always unmindful of reward and unafraid of possible failure; a man with no need for vacations; a Karmayogi with skill in works and a will to do his best, and every waiting on Grace; the lover of all loversof Tamil, without distinction of caste, creedor positon; editor of Senthamil Selvi for 55 years; a publisher with a rare knowledgeof the instruments he has to deploy, and with a talent for choosing the right people for the work on hand; a leader evoking loyalty all roundand eliciting their fullest co-operation; a visionary with a feelong for the future and its paramount needs; ‘Padma Sri’ and ‘Senthamil Selvar;; stauch adherent of aram, or dharma: such has been, and such is, Tiru VA Su, who has sustained himself woith the Kazhagam’s ideals and inspirations, and also sustained the Kazhagam with his very life-breath. Agent, Secretary Managing  Director, the designations haven’t mattered mu;ch to him, for total dedication has been the sole criterion of his sevice. While these achievem,ents undert the Kazhagam’s banner have been phenomenal enough, Va Suis without pride, and he is far from complacent. He knows that there is still a long way to go. He is a man of simple food haits, wedded to simplicity and austerity; and he can readily forget and forgive an injury. He abhors waste, idleness and gossip; and, deep within, he is strong with the strength of his religious faith and reliance on the Grace Divine. Admirers have hailed him as a university in hikself, and as a waling library, but these desfcriptions canot exhaust the Manor his work. He lightly carries the weight of his 84 years, and he has not still ceasedto hanker afterfurther goals of aspiration and achievement. He is indeed the Happy Warrior who has made his life a grand God-given opportunity for unselfish unblemished and unending service to Tamil. On this occasion I wish his many more years of active and useful life, and all joy of fulfilment in the new tasks that he may undertake.
152, Kutchery Road,	K.R.SRINIVASA IIYENGAR
Mylapore, Madras : 600004
24th August, 1981

curuṅkac collal eṉpatu pattuvakai aḻakukaḷuḷ talaiyāyatu. curukki eḻutuka eṉpatu innāḷ tērvup payiṟci kaḷuḷ oṉṟu! āṉāl, virippiṉ akalum tokuppiṉ eñcum eṉṟu kaḻakac cāṉṟōr uraittatu pōṉṟa toru peru varalāṟṟaic curukki varaivataṉ arumai eḻutat toṭaṅkiya piṉṉar naṉku teḷivāyiṟṟu!
paṉṉūl tokutikaḷāka eḻutappeṟa vēṇṭiya varalāṟu, ic ciṉṉūl aḷavil eḻutappeṟṟiruppatu uṇmai; āṉāl, varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷ aṉaittum muḻutuṟa iṭam peṟā viṭiṉum, varalāṟṟil cuṭṭa vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamaiyāp pakutikaḷ viṭupeṟavillai eṉṟē eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ; eṉiṉum, ovvoru talaippum viritteḻutattakka viḻuppamuṭaiyatē eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyamillai!
nīṭiruṅ kuṉṟam, niḻalkālum maṇṭilattuk
	kōṭu kōṭāyt tōṉṟum koḷkaittē
eṉṉum cilappatikāram toṭarpāṉa paḻam pāṭaṟ kuṟippu iv varalāṟṟukkum ēṟkum!
tamiḻaṉṉaiyiṉ nilaimakkaḷuḷ talaimakkaḷākat tōṉṟi yōr palar; avarkaḷ paṭaippāḷar; uraiyāḷar; patippāḷar; parappāḷar; kāppāḷar eṉap pala tiṟattar. aṉṉa varai ellām tamiḻkūṟu nallulakap parappellām iṉṉār eṉṟu oppārum mikkārum illā nilaiyila parappik koṇṭirukkum ēntal iv varalāṟuṭaiyār!
teruvellām tamiḻmuḻakkam ceḻikkac ceyvīr eṉṟum, tēmaturat tamiḻōcai ulakamelām paravumpakai ceytal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tiṟamāṉa pulamaiyeṉil veḷināṭṭār atai vaṇakkam ceytal vēṇṭum eṉṟum uṇarvup paṭaippāḷikaḷ kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭutal eḷitu. āṉāl avaṟṟai uṇarntu uruppaṭiyāyk kaṭamaipuriyum ceyalvīrar kāṇṭaṟku ariyar; mika mika ariyar! ivvariyarāyc ceyaṟkariya ceyyum periyarāy, aṟupāṉ āṇṭukaḷukku mēlum taṭaippāṭum iṭaippāṭum iṉṟit toṭarntu ceyalāṟṟik koṇṭuvarum uravōr ivvaralāṟṟu nāyakar eṉpatu nāṭaṟinta ceyti!
coṟkōvum tōṇipurat tōṉṟalumnam cuntaraṉum
	ciṟkōla vātavūrtti tēcikaṉum - muṟkōli
	vantilarēl nīṟeṅkē? māmaṟainūl tāṉeṅkē?
	entaipiraṉ ainteḻutteṅ kē
eṉṟu civāṉanta mālai eṅkē? eṅkē? eṅkē? eṉa viṉā eḻuppukiṉṟatu. iv varalāṟṟu nāyakar tōṉṟāviṭiṉ, tiruvaraṅkaṉārkkup patippuk kātal kiḷarntu kaḻakak kaṉi mara vittu ūṉṟātiruntirukkappeṟiṉ, ettaṉai ettaṉai eṅkēk kaḷaik kēṭpatu?
kaḻaka nūlāciriyarkaḷ ōriṭattār allar; ōriṉattār allar; oru kālattār allar; oru tuṟaiyār allar; oru nilaiyār allar; oru camayattār allar; oru pālār allar; ivarum, oru nūṟṟuvar irunūṟṟuvarallar; paṉṉūṟṟuvar! ik kaḻaka nūlāciriyarkaḷ maṭṭumō ivar toṭarpaḷār?
viḻumiya viḻāt talaimaiyēṟṟōr ettuṇair? poḻivu ceytu polivuṟuttiyōr ettuṇaiyar? aluval toṭarpāl aṇukkarāyōr ettuṇaiyar? araciyal vallunar, nītittuṟaic cāṉṟōr, kalvit tuṟai, kalait tuṟai cārntēr - ivarkaḷ ettuṇaiyar? ivarkaḷ aṉaivar nerukkamum nēyamum oṉṟuviṭāmal eḻutap pukiṉ iv varalāṟu ettuṇai virivām?
orō oru nūl veḷiyīṭṭilēyē tokkik kiṭakkum varalāṟukaḷ ettuṇaiyō uṇṭu! orō oru nūlāciriyar toṭarpilēyē ettuṇaiyō varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷ tokkik kiṭappatu uṇṭu! ivvāṟāka ēṟakkuṟaiya 1725 nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭṭa veḷiyīṭṭu vēntariṉ varalāṟṟil, veḷiyiṭṭa oru nūlukku oru pakkam eḻutuvatu eṉṟālum, innūl evvaḷavu virintu pōkum! oru pāṉai cōṟṟukku ōraviḻ patam eṉṟu iv varalāṟṟaik koḷka.
tokuppuk kalai ēntalākiya ivar, kaṭitakkalaik kāvalar eṉpataik kaṭṭāyam kuṟippiṭal vēṇṭuma. ivar eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷ ōrāyiramā? īrāyiramā? paṉṉāyiram eṉka. ik kaṭitaṅkaḷuṅku vaippup paṭikaḷ uṇṭu eṉṟāl, ivar kaṭita vaippar eṉpatileṉṉē aiyam! ivar eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷ ittuṇai eṉṟāl, ivarukku aṟiñarkaḷ eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷ ettuṇai! kaṭṭuk kaṭṭākavum, kōppuk kōppākavum vaikkappeṟṟuḷa! avai āvaṇak kāppakam pōlak kaṭitak kāppakamāka amaittaṟku uriyavai. avaṟṟai oḻuṅkuṟutti virinta varalāṟu eḻutuvatāyiṉ āyiram āyiram pakkamāka nūl viriyum. avvirinta nūl, tamiḻakattiṉ ivvirupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ varalāṟṟuk kalaik karuvūlamākat tikaḻum!
eḷiyēṉ vāḻvil iyalpākap peṟṟa ariya pēṟukaḷ cila uṇṭu. avaṟṟuḷ talaiyāya oṉṟu kaḻaka āṭciyāḷar avarkaḷiṉ kaṇipparumkaṭṭilāk kātaṉmait toṭarpu! at toṭarpāl amainta perumpēṟu, eḷiyēṉ vāḻvu, muḻutuṟum amiḻtat tamiḻ vāḻvu ākiyatēyām! iṉpat tamiḻil iṭaiyaṟātu tōyntu, eyppilā nalattai eṉṟeṉṟum eytikkoṇṭirukkum pēṟu eḷitil evarkkum vāyppatō? av vāyppiṉ iṭaiyē vāytta taṉipperum pēṟu - ceyalāṟṟutalaic ceḻuntavamākak koṇṭa ic centamiḻc celvar varalāṟṟai eḻutum pēṟākum. eṉṉeṉiṉ, iv varalāṟuṭaiyār iṭattiliruntu tatumpi vaḻiyum taṉittamiḻp paṟṟu evarkkē vāykkum? it taṉit tamiḻt toṇṭu ceyya ettuṇaip pēr muṉaintār? am muṉaippai eṉṟum ayarāmal cōrāmal muḻutuṟac celutti ettuṇaip pēr ceyalāṟṟiṉār? ivartam ceyalāṇmait tiṟattiṟku it tuṟaiyil ivarkku oppākavātal, uyar vākavātal evarē iruntār? ivaraip pōl payaṉila niṉaiyāmai, payaṉila collāmai, payaṉilu ceyyāmai evarē pārittuc cikkeṉap pōṟṟiyiruntār? ivaraippōl payaṉila evarē pārittuc cikkeṉap pōṟṟiyiruntār? ivaraip pōl maṟaimalaikkup parappunarākavum, kūṭṭuppaṅku niṟuvaṉat tiṟkuk kāṭṭunarākavum evarē iruntār? irukkiṉṟār?
paṭam pārttal, veṟṟilai pōṭutal, pukaipuṭittal, kāppi tēnīr ākiyavai kuṭittal, iḻinta pāṭalkaḷaik kēṭṭal, takavaṟṟa nūlkaḷaip paṭittal - ākiyavai ivar kaṉaviṉum karutātavai!
cīṭṭu viḷaiyāṭutal, paricuccīṭṭu naṭattutal, kutiraip pantayattiṟkup pōtal, ēmāṟṟu āṭṭaṅkaḷil īṭupaṭutal ākiyavai ivarāl veṟuttu otukkappaṭupavai!
vīṇ pēccup pēcutal, vāḷā poḻutaik kaḻittal, kēlikiṇṭal naiyāṇṭi ceytal ākiyavaṟṟil eṉṟum īṭupaṭṭaṟiyār! takkavar toṭarpai aṉṟip piṟar toṭarpai valakkāramāka vilakkuvār! vāṭṭum nōyiṉ iṭaiyēyum, ūṭṭum maruntiṉ iṭaiyēyum ōyviṉṟi uḻaippatai oru peruntavamākak koṇṭuḷḷa takavuṭaiyār! takuti vāynta paṇikaḷaittērntu ceytālvāḻum nāḷum nīḷum eṉpataip palappala cāṉṟukaḷālniṟuvik kāṭṭit tam vāḻvaiyum avvaṇṇamē uyttuc celvatāl eṉṟum iḷaiyarāy vāḻum peṟṟiyār! ic cīrmaikaḷai niṉaintu niṉaintu uṇarntu varalāṟu eḻutum pōtu, anniṉaivālum av vuṇarvālum āṭkoḷḷap peṟṟuc cārntaṉaṉ vaṇṇamāka aḻuntippōkum pēṟṟiṉum ariya pēṟu oruvarukku vēṇṭumō? attaku uyirūtiyap pēṟu tiruvaruḷ aruḷiyatē eṉpatu oru talai!
nūṟṟitaḻ alari nirai kaṇṭāṟpōla, nūṟu pallāṇṭu uraiyum pāṭṭum uṭaiyōrāy vāḻkatil eṉpatu māṇikka moḻi! ām; muṉaivar va. cupa. māṇikkaṉār ivarkku uraitta vāḻttu moḻi. am moḻiyāl moḻi vaḷarkkum ēntalai vāḻtti iv varalāṟṟup paṭaiyalai aṭiyuṟai ceytu makiḻkiṉṟēṉ.

1. oru peruviḻā
(paṉinīr palamuṟai teḷittār; cantaṉam aḷittār; mālai cūṭṭi makiḻntār; pārāṭṭeṭuttup paravaca muṟṟār; avaṟṟaiyellām oruṅkē tirumpappeṟa vāyttavēḷaiyil tikkumukkāṭip pōṉār.)
ceṉṉaiyilē oru peruviḻā. avviḻāvil araciyal talaivarkaḷum aṟiñar perumakkaḷum kuḻumiyuḷḷaṉar. viḻā nāyakarukkup pukaḻmālai cūṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.
eṉakkut terinta varaiyil tiru.va.cuppaiyāpiḷḷai avarkaḷaik kolampacukku oppiṭalām. kolampacu amerikkāvaik kaṇṭupiṭittatupōl ivarmaṉitaṉaik kaṇṭupiṭittār. tamiḻ nāṭṭil yār yārmaṉitarāka irukki ṉṟārkaḷ, eḻuttāḷarkaḷāka irukkiṉṟārkaḷ, aṟiñarāka irukkiṉṟārkaḷ, evarellām nūlāciriyarāka irukkat takutiyuḷḷavarāka irukkiṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷ. āciriyarkaḷāka illātavarkaḷai āciriyarkaḷākkiṉārkaḷ. nūlāciriyar ākkiṉārkaḷ.
oru pācuvel illaiyāṉal eppaṭi cāṉcaṉ illaiyō apōl mṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷukkup perum viḷamparattait tēṭit tantavarkaḷ tiru. cuppaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷē.
tiru. cuppaiyāpiḷḷai avarkaḷ oru puttaka vaṇikar maṭṭumallar. avar oru centamiḻk kāvalar, pulavar, puravalar, paltuṟai aṟivu parappunar: in nāṭṭu muṉṉēṟṟat toṇṭar kaḷuḷ oruvar.
avar ōr iyalpāṉa puttaka veḷiyīṭṭāḷar maṭṭu mallar; avariṭattil irukkum oruperum ciṟappu eṉṉa veṉṟāl, avarē oru ciṟanta eḻuttāḷar. avarē oru puttaka viṟpaṉaiyāḷar; avarē oru puttaka veḷiyīṭṭāḷar; avarē oru nūlakar; avarē oru vācakar; ōr itaḻāciriyarākavum irukkiṟār.
aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ inta nilaiyam tuvaṅkum poḻutu enta aḷavukkut taṉittamiḻppaṟṟāḷarāka iruntārō, antat taṉittamiḻp paṟṟil iruntu koñcam kūṭap piṟaḻntu kalapput tamiḻukku avar māṟavillai. aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉāl inta nilaiyattiṉ poṟuppai ivar ēṟkumpōtu eppaṭit tammai oppaṭaittārō, ataṟkup piṉpu entak kavarcciyum, enta atircciyum tammai māṟṟaviṭāmal itaṟkē tammai oppaṭaittuviṭṭār. ippaṭi oruvar iruppatu inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ nākarikattai mīṟiya ceyal
aimpatu āṇṭukaḷil ivaraiviṭac ciṟanta toṇṭāṟ ṟiyavarkaḷ irukka muṭiyumā?
iṟaivaṉ avarukkuk koṭuttirukkum nalla paṇpu, avarukku yārum pakaiyillai.
oruvaṉ entac camayattil iruntālum avaṉ tamiḻukku aṉpuṭaiyavaṉāka iruntāl avaṉaic caivaṉ eṉṟu karutuvārkaḷ.
tamiḻ paṭittavarkaḷ tamiḻ uṇarntavarkaḷ yārāka iruntālum nammuṭaiya piḷḷai avarkaḷaip pōlattāṉ irukka vēṇṭum.
viḻā nāyakar avarkaḷē, oru nalla nūl; anta nalla nūlukku uḷḷa poruḷ tamiḻ oṉṟutāṉ
avarkaḷ tokuttu vaittuḷḷa nūl ovvoṉṟum oru katai collum
matippukkuriya nūl nilaiyaṅkaḷē, niṟaintirukkum uṅkaḷ pēḻaikaḷil illāta, āṉāl uṅkaḷukku mika mikat tēvaiyāṉavaṟṟai nāṉēkoṇṭu varukiṟēṉ eṉṟāṉ vālṭṭu viṭmaṉ. antak kaviñaṉaip poṟuttavarai aḵtu uṇmaiyō? poyyō? nāṉaṟiyēṉ; āṉāl tamiḻaṉṉai
yiṉ eḷiya nūlnilaiyattil illāta, āṉāl iṉṟiyamai
yātu tēvaippaṭṭa eṇṇaṟṟa nūlkaḷait tiru. piḷḷai
yavarkaḷ koṇṭu tantārkaḷ
tamiḻ vāḻa vāḻnta umatu tavattai iṉṟaiya tamiḻakam uṇarntu pōṟṟukiṟatu. etirkālat tamiḻakam niṉaintu naṉṟi celuttum; tamiḻ varalāṟu umatu toṇṭiṟkut talai vaṇaṅkum.
piḷḷai avarkaḷiṉ toṇṭiṉaip paṟṟik kūṟa vēṇṭu māṉāl avai poṉṉeḻuttukkaḷāl poṟikkat takkavai eṉṟē kūṟavēṇṭum.
avarkaḷ ceytavarum tamiḻttoṇṭu etirkālattil kalveṭṭukkaḷil poṟikku maḷavukkup perumaiyuṭaiya
tākum.
uṇmaiyilēyē caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakum eṉṟāl cuppaiyāpiḷḷaitām. cuppaiyāpiḷḷai eṉṟāl caiva cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakamtāṉ.
oru ciṟappāṉa tuṇaiyai āṇṭavaṉ avarukku aḷittirukkiṟār.
- ivvāṟu aṟiñar palartāmaraic celvar va. cuppaiyā piḷḷai avarkaṭkup pukaḻāram cūṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.
viḻā nāyakar eḻupattaintāṇṭu iḷaiñar (1973 ām āṇṭil) ēṟpurai vaḻaṅkukiṉṟār. appoḻutu teṟitta maṇikaḷ;
nāṉ eppōtum iḷamaiyaittāṉ virumpuvēṉ
eṉṉai yārāvatu mutiyavar eṉṟu coṉṉāl atai nāṉ cuvaippatillai
tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟai eḻuta vēṇṭumāṉāl - tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ varalāṟṟai eḻuta vēṇṭumāṉāl - eṉ tamaiyaṉār tiruvaraṅkar varalāṟṟaittāṉ mutaṉ mutalil eḻuta vēṇṭum. eṉ tamaiyaṉār illāviṭṭāl maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷāriṉ perumai ivvaḷavu viḷakka muṟṟirukkātu eṉpatai nāṉ aṟivēṉ.
tamiḻukku eṉṉa ceyyalām, tamiḻarkku varukiṟa iṭaiyūṟu kaḷai eppaṭip pōkkalām - ivaiyē eṉakku eppōtum cintaṉai.
ōyvu oḻiccal eṉpavai eṉakkup piṭikkātavai
eṉakkuk kiṭaikkappeṟum pukaḻukkum perumaikkum uriyavarkaḷ eṉ arumait tamaiyaṉārtiruvaraṅkaṉār avarkaḷum, taṉit tamiḻt tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārum āvar
eṉakku viḻā eṭuppatai nāṉ virumpuvatillai. āṉāl inta viḻā ēṉ eṭukkappaṭṭatu eṉṟāl, nāṉ eppaṭiyellām perumpulavarkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭēṉ eṉpatu avar kaḷukkut teriyavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākavē. nūlāciriyarkaḷuk kellām eḻuti avarkaḷ karuttai aṟiyacceytu ataṉ mūlam avarkaḷum eṉṉaippōl perumakkaḷuṭaiya aṉpaiyum ātaravaiyum peṟavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākavum, eṉṉaippōl avarkaḷum ūkkam peṟavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākavumē iv viḻā eṭukkappaṭṭatē aṉṟi eṉatu perumaiyait terivippa taṟkāka aṉṟu.
ip poḻivu maṇikaḷum, ēṟpurai maṇikaḷum mēlvarum varalāṟṟiṉ mēlvaric caṭṭam pōlvaṉavām.


2. oru maṉitar
(oruvaṉ taṉ tōḷilēyē taṉ piḷḷaiyai vaittuk koṇṭu ataṉait terut teruvākat tēṭiṉāṉ. atupōl ulakam maṉitaṉait taṉṉarukil vaittuk koṇṭē maṉitaṉait tēṭi eṅkeṅkō alaikiṉṟatu.) 
paṭṭappakalilē oruvar tam talaimēl viḷakkait tāṅkik koṇṭu vantār. teruvilpōkiṉṟavarum varukiṉṟa varum, pakalilē viḷakkēntippōkum intap paittiyattaip pār eṉap pēci nakaittaṉar. oruvar periyavarē, pakalilē ēṉ viḷakkēntikkoṇṭu alaikiṟīr? eṉa viṉāviṉār. nāṉ maṉitaṉaittēṭi alaikiṟēṉ eṉṟār anta maṉitar. tayācīṉacu eṉpatu avar peyar. maṉitarai maṉitar tēṭuvatum, maṉitaraimaṉitar kaṇṭu koṇṭāṭuvatum ulakil kāṇum nikaḻccikaḷē.
viḷakkēntikkoṇṭu vītiyilē alaintu maṉitarait tēṭiyatu mēlaināṭu. tamiḻnāṭu, tiṇṇaiyilēyiruntu maṉitaraik kaṇṭu kaḷippuṟṟatu.
tiruvoṟṟiyūril tēraṭit teruttiṇṇaiyil iruntār oru tuṟavi. āṅku iruntukoṇṭu, pōkum makkaḷaip pārttu avaravar taṉmaikkut takkavāṟu atu pōkiṟatu, itu pōkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭiruntār. av vaḻiyāka oru nāḷ vaḷḷalār varak kaṇṭār tuṟavi; paṇivōṭu eḻuntār; uyarntamaṉitar varukiṉṟār eṉṟār. pēraruḷ perukkamē ōruruvākit toṇṭilē tulaṅkum vaḷḷalārē uyarnta maṉitar eṉak koṇṭār, at tuṟavi. maṉitarai maṉitar kaṇṭu koṇṭār.
maṉitappiṟavi eṭuttālum maṉitarākak kāṭciyaḷit
tālummaṉitattaṉmai illatavarum ulakil vāḻtal kaṇkūṭu. tiruvaḷḷuvar ittakaiyavarkaḷai, makkaḷē pōlvar kayavar eṉṟār. pāratiyār, vēṭikkai maṉitar eṉṟār. av vēṭikkai maṉitar iyalpukaḷai avar virittuk kūṟiṉār.
tēṭic cōṟunitam tiṉṟu - pala
		ciṉṉañ ciṟukataikaḷ pēci - maṉam
	vāṭit tuṉpamika uḻaṉṟu - piṟar
		vāṭap palaceyalkaḷ ceytu - narai
	kūṭik kiḻapparuvam eytik - koṭuṅ
		kūṟṟuk kiraiyeṉappiṉ māyum - pala
	vēṭikkai maṉitar.
aritil ellām aritām māṉiṭappiṟaviyai ūṇukkum, veṭṭip poḻutu pōkkukkum, veṟukkum ceyalkaḷukkumē urimaiyākkiyavar vēṭikkai maṉitar eṉpatil viyappillaiyē! atē poḻutil, eḷḷaittaṉaippoḻutum payaṉiṉṟi irātu, tiṭṭamiṭṭa vakaiyilē tiruttoṇṭu ceypavar maṉitaruḷ maṉitar eṉpatilum viyappillaiyē!
maṉitaruḷ maṉitarāka oruvarai ākkupavar yār? avarē tammai ākkikkoḷḷal vēṇṭum. nalla nilaiyilē tammai niṟuttupavarum, annilaiyilē iruntutammait tāḻttupavarum, tām irukkum nilaiyilē iruntu, mēlum mēlum uyarttik koṇṭu pōpavarum, evarkkum talaimaiyāṉavarākat tammaic ceypavarum tāmēyaṉṟip piṟarallar eṉap pēcum oru nālaṭippāṭṭu. innilai evarkku uṇṭām? tirumūlar itaṟkut teḷivāṉa viṭai pakarkiṟār :
eṉṉai naṉṟāka iṟaivaṉ paṭaittaṉaṉ
	taṉṉai naṉṟākat tamiḻceyu māṟē
eṉpatu atu.
iṟaivaṉ paṭaippiṉ nōkkattaip purintukoṇṭu, itaṟkākavē eṉṉaip paṭaittāṉ; avviṟaivaṉ paṭaippiṉ nōkkattai eṉṉāl evvaḷavu iyalumō avvaḷavu aritiṉ muyaṉṟu niṟaivēṟṟutalē eṉ piṟavippayaṉ eṉakkoṇṭu pāṭupaṭupavarē paṇpaṭṭa maṉitar! ittakaiyavaraiyē vaiyattuḷ vāḻvāṅku vāḻpavar eṉpār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
perumai mikka maṉitarāka vāḻpavar evarō avar, tām piṟanta maṇṇukkum, tām piṟanta iṉattukkum, tām irunta kālattukkum, tām ceyyum toḻilukkum perumai cērkkiṉṟaṉar. itkaiyōr perumaiyē, etirkāla māntarāl eṭuttukkāṭṭākak koḷḷum ēṟṟam peṟukiṟatu. ivarkaḷ ceyta toṇṭu ellai kaṭanta āyiram āyiram āṇṭu kaḷukkum eṇṇippārppatāyp payaṉpaṭuvatāy irukkum. ivvakaiyil, tamiḻmoḻiyum, tamiḻnāṭum, tamiḻ iṉamum uḷḷavarai uḷḷavarāy vāḻum perumaiyait tammōṭu cērttukkoṇṭa peruntakai tāmaraic celvar
va. cuppaiyāpiḷḷai āvar. tamiḻp palkalaik kaḻakam oṉṟait tōṟṟuvikkāta tamiḻakat tīyūḻukku iṭaiyē nallūḻāka vāyttavar at tiruvāḷar! avar vāḻvārāka! maṉitarait tēṭit tēṭikkaṇṭu, mūlaiyil iruntārai muṟṟattil vaitta cālap periya rām avar neṭitūḻi vāḻvārāka!

3. perumakaṉār piṟantār
(piṟakkumpōtē maruvum maṇakkum; marukkoḻuntum maṇakkum; tuḷaciyum maṇakkum; āṉāl, tuḷaci maṇamperukku vatuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭu poruḷākavum perumai peṟṟuviṭukiṟatu.)
tamiḻccollukkut taṉicciṟappu uṇṭu. ovvoru collaiyum uṉṉippāka nōkkiṉāl, aḵtu oru varalāṟṟu ōviyamāka viḷaṅkum. makaṉ makaḷ eṉṉum coṟkaḷ ac coṟkuṭaiyār uyara uyara māṉ, māḷ eṉa uyarntu viṭukiṉṟatu. perumakaṉ perumāṉ ākiṉṟāṉ. perumakaḷ perumāḷ ākiṉṟāḷ! peṇperumāḷ vēṇmāḷ mutaliya peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷai aṟivōr perumāḷ eṉpatu peṇpāṟ peyar eṉpatai aiyuṟār:
akal āl ākavillaiyā? akaṉṟa pulamaiyum, akaṉṟa uḷamum uṭaiya akaṉṟōr āṉṟōr allarō! ivvāṟē perumakaṉ perumāṉ ākiṉṟāṉ; cēkkiḻār perumāṉ cīraṭi pōṟṟi, vaḷḷuvar perumāṉ vaḷarpukaḻ pāṭi, civaperumāṉ ceḻuṅkaḻal vāḻvē vāḻvākak koṇṭa perumakaṉ cuppaiyā piḷḷai tōṉṟiṉār. tōṉṟiṟ pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟuka! eṉpataṟku ilakkiyamākat tōṉṟiṉār: perumakaṉ, perumāṉ āṉār!
centamiḻum cantaṉamum ticaiyellām parimaḷikkap pukaḻ maṇakkum tirunāṭu porunaināṭu. annāṭṭiṟku aṇiceyyu tirunakar nellai eṉappeṟum tirunelvēli. ataṟkuk kīḻpāl, peruki vaḷarnta pērūr pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭai. ap pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaiyilē piṟantār tiru.va.cu.
maturaiyai aṭuttu vaṭapāl mōkūr eṉṉum paḻaimaiyāṉatōr ūr uḷatu. avvūraip paḻanāḷil āṭciceytavaṉ paḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉ eṉpāṉ. avaṉ iḷaiyōṉ iḷam paḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉ eṉpāṉ. avaṉ, kōṭṭaiyeḻuppik kuṭiyamaittuk kōlōcciya iṭam paḻaiyaṉ kōṭṭai. ap paḻaiyaṉ kōṭṭai piṉṉai nāḷil paḷaiyaṉ kōṭṭaiyāyp, pāḷaiyaṅ kōṭṭaiyāyp, pāḷai yāḷ maruviyuḷatu. caṅkappulavar perumakkaḷ pāṭupukaḻukku uriyavaṉāka viḷaṅkiya paḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉ ūrilē caṅkanūṟpēḻai taravanta perumakaṉār piṟantatu niṉaivukūrat takkatām.
pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaiyil vāḻnta tiruvāḷar oruvar vayira muttup piḷḷai eṉpār. avartam arumaik kiḻattiyār cuntarattammaiyār. vayiramum, muttum aḻakoṭum viḷaṅkuvatai evarē aṟiyār?
oṉṟaṉ kūṟu āṭai uṭuppavar āyiṉum oṉṟiṉār vāḻkkaiyē vāḻkkai eṉpār oru caṅkaccāṉṟōr. ōr āṭaiyai irukūṟu ākki uṭuttukkoṇṭāltāṉ eṉṉa? īruṭalum ōruyirum pōṉṟu vāḻum vāḻvē vāḻvu eṉak kaṇṭa avvuyil kalantu oṉṟiya uyarnta vāḻvai vayiramuttarum cuntarattammaiyum koṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ illaṟavāḻvup payaṉāka makkaḷ aivar tōṉṟiṉar.
mūttavar muttucāmi, aṭuttavar tiruvaraṅkar. mūṉṟāmavar aiyam perumāḷ, nāṉkāmavar ivvaralāṟṟu nāyakar cuppaiyā; ivarkaḷukkellām taṅkai kuppammāḷ aintāmavar. tiru. va.cu. piṟantatu 1897 ām āṇṭu cepṭampart tiṅkaḷ 22ām nāḷ ākum. tamiḻmoḻi kākka vanta talaimakaṉait tam kaṭaici makaṉākat tantār cuntarattammaiyār. kaṭaippaṭṭār talaippaṭṭār eṉṉum paḻamoḻi meyyāyiṟṟu. aṉṟiyum tamiḻ aṉṉai ceyta tavapperun tirunāḷum annāḷ āyiṟṟu!


4. iḷamaiyum kalviyum
(iḷamai, kalvikkuriyatu; āṉāl, av viḷamai etta ṉaiyō iṭarkaḷaic cantikka vēṇṭiyatākavum amaintu viṭukiṟatu. av viṭar veḷḷattil etir nīccal aṭikka vallavarkku av viḷamai eṉṉa ceykiṉṟatu? eṉṟum iḷamaiyāy irukkum pēṟṟai aruḷiviṭukiṟatu.)
makkaḷiṉ kalvi tantaiyaraic cērntatu eṉpatu muntaiyōr muṟai. tantaiyoṭu kalvipōm eṉṟu avarkaḷ urattukkūṟiyatum uṇṭu.
vayiramuttuppiḷḷai tam makkaḷukkuk kalvitarum vēṭkaiyil munti niṉṟār. muyaṉṟum ceytār. Mdhš mjid K‰WÉ¡f mt® ïUªjhšjhnd Koí«?ōṭum uyirai niṟutti vaikka evarāltām muṭikiṉṟatu? vayiramuttupa piḷḷai 1900 ām āṇṭu piṟaimuṭip perumāṉ tiruvaṭi yaṭaintār.
nalliḷamaip paruvattilēyē nāyakaṉai iḻantār cuntarattammaiyār. avar eṉṉa ceyvār? tamakku nērnta tīyūḻai eṇṇit terumantu nōvārā? patiṉmūṉṟāṇṭu muttucāmiyaiyum, pattāṇṭut tiruvaraṅkaraiyum, ēḻāṇṭu aiyam perumāḷaiyum, mūṉṟāṇṭuc cuppaiyāvaiyum, ōrāṇṭum nirampāta kuppammāḷaiyum eṇṇi eṇṇip puṇpaṭuvārā? tampiyar uḷarāka vēṇṭum eṉṉum taṉipperum tayaiyāl uyir vāḻnta tilakavatiyār pōlat tam iḷañcelvaṅkaḷiṉ etirkāla vāḻvai nōkki, ampoṉmaṇi nūl tāṅkāmal aruḷ tāṅki niṉṟār.
āliṉ aṭimaram kaṟaiyāṉāl arikkappeṟṟu vīḻntālum, maram vīḻntu paṭāmal, ataṉ vīḻtukaḷ kākkum. atupōl tantaiyillāk kuṭumpattait tāṅka muṟpaṭṭār mūttavar muttucāmi, avarkkut tantaiyār ceytuvanta vaṇikattil ōraḷavu muṉṉarē payiṟciyiruntatu. ap payiṟciyum ārvamum kaikoṭuttu utava vaṇikattil īṭupaṭṭār. tiṟamum tērcciyum mikkavarkaḷ ceyyum vaṇikamē tiṭumeṉṟu cāyntuviṭak kāṇkiṟōm. ivviḷaiñarāl pōṭṭimikka vaṇika ulakil īṭukoṭukka iyalumā? iyalāmal pōkavē vaṇikam iḻappil muṭintatu.
poruḷ eṉṉum celvac cevili iruntāl kuṭumpam kuṟaiviṉṟi naṭakkum. tāṅkuvārum tarippārum palarāka varuvar. talaivar illā vīṭu, irunta poruḷum iḻappukku ākiya kuṭumpam; innilaiyil eṉṉa ceyya muṭiyum? koṭitu koṭitu vaṟumai koṭitu; ataṉiṉum koṭitu iḷamaiyil vaṟumai eṉṟu pulampalām. uṇṭākiya paḷḷattai mēṭākka vakai vēṇṭumē!
paṭippilē pērārvam uṭaiya araṅkaṉār tam paṭippai mūṉṟām paṭiva aḷavilē viṭuttu vēlai tēṭiṉār. vaḻakkuraiñar oruvariṭam eḻuttar vēlaiyil amarntār. av vēlaiyil araṅkarkku īṭupāṭu ēṟpaṭavillai. vēṟu vēlai tēṭit tūttukkuṭikkuc ceṉṟār. āṅkirunta vaḻutūr aḻakiya cuntarampiḷḷai eṉpār tuṇaiyiṉāl kappal ēṟik koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟār. āṅkirunta eḻuttar paṇiyil amarntār. itu nikaḻntatu 1907 ām āṇṭil ākum.
tamaiyaṉār iruvarum tam ayarā uḻaippāl kuṭumpap poṟaiyait tāṅkikkoḷḷavē, iḷaiyavar va.cu. pāḷaiyiluḷḷa tūya cavēriyār uyarpaḷḷiyil cērntu payiṉṟār. viḷaiyum payir muḷaiyilē teriyum eṉpataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu kalviyil karuttuṭaiyavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār.
periya kuṭumpattait tāy tāṅki niṟkum perumai; ak kuṭumpa varuvāykkākak kalviyai viṭuttum toḻiṟ kaṭamaiyilē īṭupaṭṭa mūttavarkaḷ nalluḷḷam; iyalpilē tamakku amainta paṇpāṭu -ivaiyellām iḷamparuvattilēyē va.cu.vaic ciṟup periyār ākkiviṭṭaṉa. ātalāl kālam tavaṟāmai, kaṭamaiyil kaṇṇāka iruttal, oḻukkattāl uyartal ākiyavai yellām oruṅkē vaḷara vaḷarntu vantār. itaṉāl āciriyarkaḷ pārāṭṭukku uriyavarāy viḷaṅkiṉār.
āṇṭutōṟum tērvukaḷil cīruṟat tēṟi vakuppil ciṟappiṭam peṟṟār; kaṇakkup pāṭattilum poḻippāṭaṅ kaḷilum iṇaiyaṟṟa tiṟam kāṭṭip paricukaḷ peṟṟār.
kalviyil īṭupāṭu kāṭṭum cilar vīṭṭukku utaviyāka iruppatu illai. āṉāl va.cu. aṉṉaikku evvaḷavu utaviyāka irukka muṭiyumō avvaḷavu utaviyāka iruntār. vīṭṭup poṟuppukaḷai viruppoṭu ceytār. vītikkuc ceṉṟu veṭṭip poḻutu pōkkuvatō araṭṭaiaṭittup poḻutai vīṇākkuvatō iḷam paruvattiliruntē ivar aṟiyātavai.
poḻutai vīṇpaṭuttāta nalla vaḻakkam vāḷā oḻiyumō? poruḷ vakaiyilum cikkaṉamāka irukkavum, ciṟu ciṟu tokaiyaiyum cēmittu vaikkavum tūṇṭutalāka amaintatu. koḻumpil iruntu arumait tamaiyaṉāraṉuppum paṇam ivar peyarukkē varum. vīṭṭukkuc cērkka vēṇṭiya tokaiyai vīṭṭukkuc cērppatuṭaṉ, tamakkeṉat taṉiyē varum tokaiyaiyum kaṇakkiṭṭuc cikkaṉamākac celaviṭṭār.
cikkaṉap paṇpiṟku aṇṇal kāntiyaṭikaḷaiyum tantai periyāraiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭākak kūṟuvar. avvāṟē ovvoru ciṟuvakaiyilum cikkaṉattaip pōṟṟiya cemmal
va. cu. āvar. iṉṟiyamaiyāt tēvaikkē aṉṟip piṟitu etaṟkum celavaḻittalaic cālpākak koḷḷāta cāṉṟāṇmai iḷamai toṭṭē iruntamaiyāla, ivar tam vāḻvil tīyavai evaiyum pukāmal taṭuttāṭkoḷḷum tiruppēṟu vāykkap peṟṟār.
iḷamaiyilē iccīriya cālpu ciṟantōṅkak kāraṇamāka iruntavai palappala. avaṟṟuḷ talaiyāya oṉṟu, ovvoru nāḷum tavaṟāmal varavu celavu eḻutum naṟpaḻakkamākum. poruḷiyal aṟiñarkaḷ valiyuṟuttum im mutal tiṭṭattai mutaṉmaiyākak koṇṭu evarum ōtuvikkāmal uṇarntu kaṭaippiṭittatu av viḷamparuvattil ceyaṟkarum ceyal eṉpatai evarum aṟivar.
kalvit tiṟattilum, paṇpāṭṭilum ciṟantu vaḷarnta
va. cu. 1916 ām āṇṭu paḷḷi iṟutit tērvil veṟṟi peṟṟār. kallūriyil kāl vaikka virumpiṉār. āṉāl pollāp poruḷmuṭai vallāṇmai kāṭṭit taṭuttatu. appōtu vāyppāka irunta añcalakap paṇiyil cēra virumpiṉār. āṉāl aṇṇal araṅkaṉār pēruḷḷam vēṟāka iruntatu. tam kalviyaippōl tampiyār kalviyum taṭaippaṭṭu viṭutal ākātu eṉa eṇṇiṉār. ataṟkut takka tiṭṭamiṭṭār. tampiyaik koḻumpukku varumāṟu eḻutiṉār. iḷaval uḷḷamum aṇṇalār eṇṇattai uvantu ēṟṟu āṅkuc cella muntiyatu.
1916 ām āṇṭu cūlait tiṅkaḷ va.cu. koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟār. āṅkirunta mutaṉilaik kallūriyil paṭikkat toṭaṅkiṉār. ilaṇṭaṉ mānakara āciriyarkallūri naṭattum tērvu eḻutuvataṟku ak kallūri payiṟci tarum. at tērvu eḻuti veṟṟi peṟṟōr, maruttuvak kallūriyil cērtaṟku vāyppu uṇṭu. ātalāl at tērvu peṟutaṟkup pērārvam koṇṭār va. cu. araṅkaṉār ārvamō tām peṟāp pēṟṟait tampiyarēṉum peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa muṉaippilē iruntatu. āṉāl, toṭarntu payilvataṟku iyalāmal oru kuṟukkīṭu uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
ilaṅkai vāḻvu va.cu. uṭalnilaikku ēṟṟuk koḷḷa villai. nīrkkōvai uṇṭāki nīṅkāmal vāṭṭiyatu. ōyāt taṭima nōyuṭaṉ evvaḷavu kālamtāṉ pōrāṭa muṭiyum? muṭiyāmal koḻumpil iruntu pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaikkē tirumpiṉār.
eṭutta paṇikkut taṭaiyuṇṭāṉāl palar cōrntu viṭuvar. vāḻvilē ericcalum veṟuppum aṭaivar. āṉāl, koḷkaiyil uṟutiyāka niṉṟa va. cu. cōrntuviṭavillai. uṟutiyil taḷaravillai. uriya pāṭaṅkaḷai yellām tam ūril iruntu koṇṭē payiṉṟār. muyaṟci meyvaruttak kūli tarāmal pōkātē!
pāḷaiyil iruntē payiṉṟa va.cu. tērvukkuriya kālattil mīṇṭum koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟār. tērvait tiṟamāka eḻutiṉār. orē muṟaiyil tērcciyum peṟṟār. immuṟai koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟatu ivartam piṟavip payaṉai nilaipeṟuttat takka oru vāyppāka amaintatu.
araṅkaṉār koḻumpukkuc cellu muṉṉarē tūttukkuṭi aḻakiya cuntarampiḷḷai avarkaḷāl maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷāraip paṟṟik kēḷvippaṭṭiruntār. ataṉpiṉṉē aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya ariya nūlkaḷait taruvittuk kaṟṟu vantār. aṭikaḷāriṉ civattoṇṭukkum, centamiḻttoṇṭukkum tam uḷḷattaip paṟikoṭuttu meyyaṭiyārāka viḷaṅkiṉār. aṭikaḷāraik koḻumpukku aḻaittu avartam vāyurai kēṭkavum peruviruppāyiṉār. ataṉāl aṭikaḷārai āṅku aḻaittār.
aṭikaḷār araṅkaṉāraik koḻumpilē 1914 ām āṇṭil mutaṟkaṇ kaṇṭār. kaḻipēruvakai yaṭaintār. iḷamparuvac cevviyum, vaḷamperukiya uḷḷamum, payiṉ ṟoḻukum paṇpāṭum, tāḷāṇmai mikka vēḷāṇmaiyum, tammāṭṭuk koṇṭuḷḷa māṟāta aṉpum aṭikaḷāraik kavarntaṉa. viḻā mēṭaiyilēyē aṟupattu mūvar nāyaṉmār kaḷai yāṉ aṟivēṉ. āṉāl iṅkē 64 āvatu nāyaṉār uṇmaiyai aṟintu aḷavilā makiḻvuṟṟēṉ eṉṟu kūṟi avar ivarē eṉṟu araṅkaṉārai avaiyiṉarkku aṟimukap paṭutti viyappil āḻttiṉār. araṅkaṉārai muṉṉarē aṟintiruntavarkaḷ āmām eṉa ēṟṟaṉar. putitāka aṟintōr puṉmuṟuvar pūttu varavēṟṟaṉar. vaḷḷuvarāl pārāṭṭup peṟṟa vaḷarpukaḻ vaṇikac celvaṉpōla, aṭikaḷār pārāṭṭu araṅkaṉārkku vāyttatu.
ivvāṟu arumpi vaḷarnta aṭikaḷār araṅkar toṭarpu piṉṉē mēlum valuppeṟṟatu. aṭikaḷāriṉ nūl veḷiyīṭu viṟpaṉai ākiyavaṟṟukku utaviyatuṭaṉ perumporuḷai naṉkoṭaiyākat taṇṭiyum utaviṉār araṅkar. 1917 ām āṇṭu mīṇṭum aṭikaḷāraik koḻumpukku aḻaittār. appoḻutu iḷaval va.cu. koḻumpukkut tērvu eḻutu vataṟkākac ceṉṟirunta kālam. ātalāl, aṭikaḷār toṭarpu ivarukku vāyppatāyiṟṟu.
aṭikaḷār kulacēkarapaṭṭiṉam centilāṟumukampiḷḷai avarkaḷ vaḷamaṉaiyil taṅkiyiruntār. avarōṭu uṭaṉuṟaintu uraiyāṭi makiḻum pēṟu ivarkku uṇṭāyatu. piṉṉāḷil yāḻnūl iyaṟṟiya perumpēricaiyāciriyar vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷār annāḷil mayilvākaṉar eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ kallūri māṇavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. avaraṭikaḷār uṟaiyum vaḷamaṉaikku vantu paintamiḻ moḻi kuṟittum, centamiḻc civaneṟi kuṟittum ārvamuṟak kēṭṭum, aiyuṟṟaṉa viṉāvit teḷintum celvār. appoḻutellām uṭaṉiruntu iṉpam māntum perumpēṟu ivarkku vāyttatu. av vāyppāl aṭikaḷārmēlkoṇṭa paṟṟup perukip peruki muḻumatiyāka innāḷ viḷaṅkutal kaṇkūṭu.
ilaṇṭaṉ mānakarat tērviṉ tērcci muṭivu vantatu. atil veṟṟi peṟṟār va.cu. aṭutta muyaṟci eṉṉa? ceṉṉai maruttuvak kallūriyilcērntu payila muyaṟci mēṟkoṇṭār. 1918 ām āṇṭu cūṉ tiṅkaḷ pallāvarattiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṭikaḷār vaḷamaṉaiyil taṅkikkoṇṭu ceṉṉai maruttuvak kallūriyil cēra muyaṉṟār. am muyaṟci veṟṟitarāmaiyāl mīṇṭum pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaikkut tirumpiṉār.
paṭippārvam iṭaiyīṭu iṉṟi untiyāmaiyāl tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil iṭainilai vakuppil cērntu, iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ payiṉṟār. avvaḷavil kallūrik kalvi muṟṟuppeṟṟatu. āṉāl tāmē kaṟkum kalvikkuk kāla ellai uṇṭō? aṟitōṟu aṟiyāmai kaṇṭaṟṟu eṉṉum vaḷḷuvarvākkukku ēṟpattuṟaitōṟum puttaṟivaip perukkik koḷḷalāṉār.


5. epporuḷum āṉa tiruvaraṅkar
(uṭaṉpiṟantē kollum nōy; uṭaṉpiṟantār cuṟṟattār eṉṟirukka vēṇṭā eṉṟu tērnturaittatum inta maṇtāṉ. tampiyai iṉṟik kiṭappaṉō tamaiyaṉ maṇmēl eṉṟatum inta maṇtāṉ. taṉittaṉi nāṭi pārttu muṭivu ceyyum tamiḻ maruttuva muṟaippaṭi muṭivu ceyya vēṇṭiyatu itu.)
tēṉamarum cōlait tiruvaraṅkar epporuḷum āṉavar kāṇ ammāṉai eṉat tiruvaraṅkak kalampakam pāṭum tiruvaraṅkar, epporuḷum āṉavar eṉpatai ivarkkum kūṟattakum.
caṟṟu vaḻukkaiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya kaṉatta periya talai, nīṟu viḷaṅkum paranta neṟṟi; periya kaṇkaḷ; iṉikka iṉikkap pēcum kuṟunakaimukam; akaṉṟa mārpu; veḷḷai uṭcaṭṭai; ivaikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum palavakaikkaṭitak kaṭṭukaḷ uṭaiyatum, pottāṉ pōṭappaṭātutum ākiya mēṟcaṭṭai; iṭuppiṟ cuṟṟiyuḷḷa cempaṭṭu, kaṇukkālukku mēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷai vēṭṭi; aintaṭi uyaram; nimirntu etirnōkki naṭakkum vīranaṭai - ivai tiruvaraṅkariṉ ōviyam.
tiruvaraṅkar ārvam aṭikaḷāraik koḻumpukku aḻaittatōṭu niṉṟatō? avarkku aṇukkarāki niṟkum aḷavil amaintatō? illai! illai! avar nūlkaḷait taruvittu viṟkavum, avar iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭutaṟku ākum vakaiyil poruḷ taṇṭi viṭukkavum āka vaḷarntatu aṉṟō! ammaṭṭō? aṭikaḷāriṉ nūlkaḷai viṟṟu utavutaṟ keṉṟē oru viṟpaṉai nilaiyattaik koḻumpil toṭaṅkavum av vuḷḷaṉpu tūṇṭiyatu. aṉpu eṉṉatāṉ ceyyātu?
araṅkaṉāriṉ keḻutakai naṇparvi. caṅkaranārāyaṇa piḷḷai eṉpār. tam peyaraiyum avar peyaraiyum iṇaittuk tirucaṅkar kampeṉi (tiruvaraṅkar caṅkaranārāyaṇar kampeṉi) eṉṉum niṟuvaṉattai 1917 il niṟuviṉār. ataṉ kiḷai nilaiyattai 1918 il ceṉṉaiyilum tōṟṟuvittār. in niṟuvaṉamē caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam tōṉṟutaṟku vāytta muṉṉōṭiyāka amaintatu. aṉṟiyum aṭikaḷāriṉ nūlkaḷ cīriya vakaiyil veḷippaṭṭuc centamiḻar celvamāka viḷaṅkutaṟku vakaiyum ceytatu. aṭikaḷāriṉ ārāyccik karuvūlamāṉa tiruvācaka virivurai in niṟuvaṉattiṉ vaḻiyākavē centamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉṉum peyaril mutaṟkaṇ veḷip paṭṭatu eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
tiruvaraṅkar iyalpilēyē kūrnta mūḷaiyar; māntar iyalpai ūṭuruvi nōkka valla āṟṟal paṭaittavar; muyaṟci, cuṟucuṟuppu, kaṭaippiṭi, iṉimai,. oppuravu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ taḻumpēṟiyavar; perumakkaḷiṭam naṭpāṭum pāṅkum avar kaḷiṭam paṇikoḷḷum pāṉmaiyum kaivarap peṟṟavar. ep paṇiyaiyum arumaiyuṭaittu eṉṟu ayartal aṟiyātavar; kaṇkāṇippiṟ kaṇṭippum, pakaiyil viḻippum, paḻamaiyil kaṇṇōṭṭamum kaviṉap peṟṟavar.
kaṭitam varaitalil kālam tavaṟāk karuttuṇarvāḷar. avarai av vakaiyil veṟṟi koḷvār ariyar; āṅkilam aruntamiḻ ākiya irumoḻikaḷilum tiruttamāka eḻutum tiṟalar; eḻutuma kaṭitaṅkaḷil paḻantamiḻ nūl mēṟkōḷkaḷai ūṭāṭa viṭṭu uṇarcciyūṭṭa valvar.
tokuppuk kalaiccelvar avar. caṭṭaip paikaḷ puṭaittu niṟkat tokuppuk kuṟippukaḷ, kaṭitak kaṟṟaikaḷ, aṭaittuk koṇṭu irukkum. paḻañcuvaṭikaḷ, itaḻkaḷ, tiṅkaḷ muttiṅkaḷ āṇṭu veḷiyīṭukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟait tokuttum, tokuttavaṟṟai vaṉappuṟa vakuttum, vakuttavaṟṟai vaḷamuṟap pēṇikkāttum avar tāmē eṇṇi eṇṇi iṭaiyīṭu iṉṟic ceyta ceyaṟkarum ceyalkaḷ niṉaitōṟum iṉpam payappaṉavām. iv viyaṟkait tiṟaṉālivarcērttu vaitta aruṅkalaic celvaṅkaḷ iṉṟaiya nōkkil matippil aṭaṅkutaṟku uriyaṉa alla. maṟṟoṉṟu ivartam arumperun tiṟalkaḷ ellām viñcip perukkeṭuttuk kuṉṟiṉmēl iṭṭa viḷakkāki nāṭṭukku naṟpayaṉ viḷaikka oru tampi va. cu. vai uruvākkiyatu.
an nāḷil araṅkar ceyta vaḻikāṭṭutal - avar ōtātu kaṟṟavaṟṟai uṭaṉiruntu ōti ōti iḷaval va. cu. uṇarnta pēṟṟāl, nāṭu peṟṟa nalam ēṭumaṇakka eḻutat takkatām. ātalāl tāṉ aṇṇaṉum tampiyumākiya ivarkaḷai neṭuṅkālam aṟintu nēril paḻaki aṟintavarkaḷ tamatu toṇṭu viḷaṅkutaṟkāṉa tampiyaip peṟṟa pēṟu ivarukkē vāyttatu eṉṟu uḷamārap pārāṭṭuvārāyiṉar. empiyai īṅkup peṟṟēṉ eṉṉeṉakkariya teṉṟāṉ eṉṉum vākkaik kaṭṭik kākkap piṟanta uṭaṉpiṟantār araṅkarum 
va. cu. vum eṉpatil aiyamillaiyām.


6. nūṟpatippuk kaḻakapperumai
(etirē celkiṟārē avar kōṭi kōṭiyākat tēṭi vaittuḷ celvar. āṉāl, nāṉ maṉappāṭam ceytuḷḷa ilakkiyac celvattiṟku iṇaiyākumā avar celvam?
pullaik kāṭṭikkoṇṭu pōkiṉṟavaṉait toṭarum āvaippōl, nūlait tokuttu vaittiruppāṉait tēṭi nāṉ pōkiṉṟēṉ).
- cuvaiñar iruvar coṉmoḻi.
poruḷ illārkku iv vulakam illai - uṇmai tāṉ. poruḷallavaraip poruḷākac ceyyum, poruḷalla tillai poruḷ - ituvum uṇmaitāṉ. poruḷiṉāl ākum ellām eṉpataṟkāka ap poruḷai eppaṭiyum tēṭuvatu takumā? takkavarkaḷukkut takumā?
poruḷ tēṭutaṟkuriya vāyilkaḷuḷ talaiyāyatu vāṇikam. āṉāl, ellā vāṇikaṅkaḷum ceyyattakkaṉavē eṉiṉum cāṉṟōr uḷḷam avaṟṟil īṭupaṭuvatu illai. mācu maṟuvaṟṟa perumai taruvatuṭaṉ matikkattakka payaṉaiyum nāṭṭukkut taruvatākat tam vāṇikak kaṭamai iruppatu nalameṉ aṉṉōr eṇṇi īṭupaṭuvar. poruḷ oṉṟē avarkaḷ poruṭṭāka eṇṇi īṭupaṭāta vaṇikaneṟi atu.
koḷḷaiyō koḷḷai eṉṟu kūṟumāṟu ettaṉai ettaṉai vaṇikap pakaṟkoḷḷaikaḷ, ettaṉaiettaṉai kalappaṭaṅkaḷ, kaiyūṭṭukaḷ, kaḷḷac cantaikaḷ, kaṭattalkaḷ, patukkalkaḷ ivaiyellām oru ciṟitum illāta vaṇikamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu eṉpatum uṇmaiyē!
caṅkaccāṉṟōr kūṟiyatupōlak koḷvatum mikai koḷātu, koṭuppatum kuṟaikoṭāta aṟavili vāṇikamē oruvar puriyiṉum, avar eṭuttukkoṇṭa vāṇikattaip poṟuttu avartam uḷappāṉmai veḷippaṭumām. uyir kākkum uṇavuvakai, māṉaṅkākkum uṭaivakai, uṟṟavakaiyil ellām utavum valavakaip payaṉ poruḷkaḷ, palacarakku, aḻakup poruḷkaḷ ākiya vaṇikaṅkaḷ ellām ulakiyal naṭaimuṟaikku vēṇṭuvaṉavē; mēṟkoḷḷat  takkaṉavē! ivvāṇikaṅkaḷil nērmai kāṇappeṟiṉ arumaiyām. aḵtu atil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa naṉmakkaḷ uḷḷattu ūṟṟeṭutta peruṅkuṇattiṉ veṟṟiyāl amaintatē eṉalām.
 āṉāl, ivvellā vāṇikaṅkaḷiṉum talaiyāyatu oṉṟu uṇṭu eṉiṉ, aḵtu aṟivu nūl vāṇikamē eṉat tuṇintu kūṟalām.
marattiṉ vaḷaivai nērākkavā, kallai nērāy uṭaikkavā, maṉaiyiṭattai nērāy aṟukkavā, nūlaṭittē ceyyapaṭum. atupōl maṉattiluḷḷa kōṇalkaḷai akaṟṟi nērākka vallatu aṟivu nūl. avvaṟivu nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭṭu, viṟpaṉai ceyyum vaṇikattai mēṟkoḷvatu kaṇṇoḷi illārkkuk kaṇṇoḷi vaḻaṅkum vaḷḷaṉmai pōlvatām! aṟaṅkaḷ eṉac collappaṭum, ellāvaṟṟaiyum ceyyum arumaiyilum av vaṟattiṟku nilaikkaḷamāka niṉṟu eṉṟum kākkum nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭutal miku perumaikkuriyatēyām.
nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu eṉpatilum ettakaiya puṉmaikaḷ palkiyuḷa? iḷaiñar vāḻvaik keṭuttu eṉṟum kaṭaittēṟa oṭṭā iḻivil taḷḷum kīḻnilai nūlkaḷ ettaṉai? kaiyāl toṭṭālum kaṇṇāl kaṇṭālum cavarkkāram iṭṭuk kaḻuviṉālum tūymaiyākāta kayamai nūlkaḷ ettaṉai? piṟanta mēṉip paṭaṅkaḷai veḷiyiṭṭup pētaiyar uḷḷattai vayappaṭutti aḻikkum iḻitakai nūlkaḷ ettaṉai? ivaṟṟai eḻutuvittum patippittum vāṇikam ceyvatai nūl vaṇikam eṉṟu collalum iḻivēyām. ivai ettīya vaṇikattiṉum tīya vaṇikamām. ēṉeṉil maṟṟait tīya vaṇikaṅkaḷ vaḻivaḻi uṭalaiyum poruḷaiyum maṭṭumē keṭukkum. ittīya vaṇikamō, uyirppaiyē oṭukkuvatu! uyirppai oṭukkiya piṉṉē uyvētum uṇṭō? yātumillaiyatām!
aṟivunūl vāṇikattil talaippaṭuvārkkut taṉit takutikaḷ vēṇṭum piṟa vāṇikaṅkaḷukkup poruḷum muyaṟciyum iruppiṉ veṟṟiyām. āṉāl aṟivu nūl vāṇikam ceytaṟku aṟivuk kūrppum, aṟavuṇarvum, aruṭperukkum iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭattakkavai. oru paḷḷi āciriyaṉ ceyyum piḻai ap paḷḷiyaḷavil kēṭu ceytu ceytu amaiyum. āṉāl, oru nūlāciriyaṉ ceyyum piḻai nāṭṭaiyē nalivikkum eṉṉum peru neñcam vāynta perumakkaḷē ittuṟaiyil talaippaṭat takkavarkaḷ. ivvakaiyāl nōkkiṉ oru palkalaikkaḻakap paṇiyiṉum mēmpaṭṭa paṇiyeṉṟu collattakkatu aṟivunūl patippakappaṇi eṉṟu malaimēl ēṟiniṉṟu talainimirntu kūṟuttakka taṉipperumaiyuṭaiyatām.
puḻuvāyp pūcciyāy vīḻntu maṭiya iruppārai, iruciṟaku, eḻuppa eḻumpum paṟavaipōla uyarattiṉ uyarattil paṟakka viṭuvatu etuvō, nilaipeṟā vāḻvai nilaipeṟutta vallatu etuvō, nampikkai iḻantupōy naliyum vāḻvai, nampikkaiyūṭṭit taṭṭi niṟutti nallapala ceytaṟku ēvuvatu etuvō, toyvillāt tuṇivum, eḻucciyum toṭarntu taravallatu etuvō, mācukaḷai akaṟṟi maṇiyāka oḷiviṭac ceyya vallatu etuvō, kōṭṭattai nimirtti nērākka vallatu etuvō. koḷkaiyil piṟaḻāk koḻumaiyaik koṇṭoḻuka vaippatu etuvō, ivaṉ cāṉṟōṉ eṉṟu pallāyiravar iṭaiyē iruntālum oruvaṉai iṉaṅkaṇṭu talaivaṇaṅkac ceyyum takavaḷippatu etuvō atuvē aṟivu nūl ātaliṉ, attaku nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭum niṟuvaṉat toṇṭukku iṇai atuvēyeṉṟu kūṟuvatu aṉṟivēṟoṉṟu kūṟutaṟku illaiyām.
iṉṉum aṟivunūl veḷiyīṭē aṟappaṇiyāy irukka, appaṇiyāl aṭaiyavarum ūtiyattil cempātiyai aṟavaḻi kaḷukkē celaviṭa vēṇṭumeṉa varampukaṭṭi vāykkāl kōli aṟappayirai vaḷarttu varutaṟkut tiṭṭam tīṭṭiyatum, ataṉaic cikkeṉak kaṭaippiṭittuc cīriya aṟaniṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟait tōṟṟuvittuk kaṇṇiṉ maṇiyāyk kāttu varuvatum, poṉ malarukkup putumaṇamum vāyttatu pōṉṟa perumaikku uriyatām. ivaṟṟaiyellām eṇṇi uṇarvōr, iṟṟaikku aṟupatu yāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉē caivacittāntak kaḻakakkūṭṭup paṅku niṟuvaṉam tōṟṟuvitta arumaiyai niṉaintu viyantu pōṟṟāmal irār. kūṭṭuṟavu amaippu muṟaiyilum, puttaka veḷiyīṭṭu niṟuvaṉat taṉmaiyilum ic caivacittāntakkaḻakam taṉṉikaraṟṟa taṉic ciṟappāl, ulakukku oru māmaṇiyāy viḷaṅkutalai ivvaralāṟṟiṉkaṇ āṅkāṅku aṟintu makiḻka.


7. kaḻakak kālkōḷ
(ōṭattai ōṭṭutaṟkākat tuṭuppu valikka muṉaintāṉ avaṉ. āṉāl, oru kappaṟkāraṉ tāṉē avaṉait tēṭivantu ak kappalukku mīkāmaṉ ākki viṭṭāṉ.)
nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattiṉ ciṟappai aṟintōm. nūṟpatippuk kaḻakac ciṟappu eṉpatu eṉṉa? ataṉait tōṟṟuvittuk kaṭṭik kāppavar ciṟappēyaṉṟō!
koḻumpilē tōṉṟiya tirucaṅkar kampeṉi ōrāṇṭē āṅku naṭaipeṟṟatu. an niṟuvaṉattiṉ mēl araṅkarkku uṇṭākiya paṟṟum, toḻil tiṟamum ataṉaiyē tam vāḻvuk kaṭamaiyākak koḷḷa ēviyatu. ātalāl ataṉait tāyt tamiḻakattilē niṟuvip paṇiceyvatē muṟaimaiyum kaṭamaiyumām eṉa muṭivu ceytār. muṭivu oṉṟu eṭuttuviṭṭāl uṭaṉē atil muḻumūccāka iṟaṅkip paṇiceyvatu avar taṉmai. ātalāl koḻumpil iruntu pāḷaikku vantār. āṅkiruntu ceṉṉaikkuc ceṉṟār. aṭikaḷāruṭaṉ kalantu uraiyāṭiṉār. piṉṉarc ceṉṉai, pavaḻakkāratteru 2 āmeṇ (putu eṇ 87) kaṭṭiṭattil irunta teruppakkattu aṟaiyil tirucaṅkar kampeṉiyait toṭaṅkiṉār.
tirucaṅkar kampeṉiyaic ceṉṉaiyil toṭaṅkiya aṉṟē pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaiyil uḷḷa tam illattil ataṉ kiḷai nilaiyam toṭaṅkappeṟṟatu. araṅkar ceṉṉai nilaiyap poṟuppaip pārttuk koṇṭār. iḷaval va.cu. kiḷainilaiyap poṟuppai ēṟṟuk koṇṭār. tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭa ellaiyilum, teṉ ellaiyilum tirucaṅkar kampeṉi orēvēḷaiyilirukiḷaikaḷai virittatu. atu tamiḻ ulaka muḻuvatum taṇṇiḻal parapputaṟkuriya oru peruvāyppu uṇṭātaṟkut tūṇṭukōlāyiṟṟu. nāḷ ceyvatai nallavar ceyyār eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. nāḷum nallavarum cērntu ceytālnaṭavātaṉavum naṭakkum; nikaḻātaṉavum nikaḻum!
aṭikaḷārkku aṉparāy nellaiyilē tiru. mā. tiraviyam piḷḷai eṉpār oruvar tikaḻntār. avar aṭikaḷāraip paṉmuṟai nellaikku aḻaippittuc coṉ maḻai poḻiyac ceytār. aṭikaḷāriṉ aṟivup payir vaḷarccikku avar tiraviyamākavum iruntār. avarkku aṉpar tiru. ce. vicuvanāta piḷḷai eṉpār. avar tamiḻum caivamum taḻaittōṅka vēṇṭum eṉpatil tīrākkātalar. nammakkaḷ ettaṉaiyō putupputut toḻilmuṟaikaḷil īṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar. uyirkku ūtiyamām. ippaṇiyil īṭupaṭuvār ,larē eṉṉum eṇṇam uṭaiyavarāka iruntār. tam karuttait tiruviyam piḷḷaiyiṉiṭam veḷiyiṭṭār. tiraviyampiḷḷaiaṭikaḷār tuṇaiyai avāviṉār. aṭikaḷāreṇṇam araṅkarmēl paṭintatu.
itaṉai itaṉāl ivaṉmuṭikkum eṉṟāyntu
ataṉai avaṉkaṇ viṭal.
eṉpataṟku ēṟpa aṭikaḷār araṅkarai aṇukumāṟu āṟṟuppaṭuttiṉār. aṭikaḷārvaḻivantaavvāṟṟuppaṭai aruntamiḻ araṅkarkku aḷappilā āṉantattēṉāka amaintatu.
tiraviyampiḷḷai, vaḻakkuraiñar oruvariṭam eḻutta
rākap paṇi ceytu vantavar. teṉṉintiya vaṅki vitikaḷait tiṟamāka amaittut tantavar avar. ākaliṉ kaḻaka vitikaḷai arumaiyāy amaitr. appoḻutu uṭaṉiruntu utaviṉār iḷavalār va. cu. cīriya māḷikai eḻupputaṟku muṉṉē varaiyum varaipaṭattil aṉṟō ataṉ amaippum aḻakum arumaiyum vaḷarcci vāyppum ellām aṭaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.āliṉ vittil ap perumarap pakutiyellām aṭaṅkik kiṭattal pōla, amaippu vitikaḷilētām aṭaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa, oruniṟuvaṉattiṉ ceyaṉmuṟaikaḷ eṉpatu uṇmaiyām. itaṉai etirkāla vaḷarccik kaṇṇōṭṭattōṭu vakutta maikkat tuṇai niṉṟār, va. cu. eṉpatu avviḷamaikkē perumai taruvatām. tiṭṭamiṭṭut terintamainta kaḻaka vitimuṟai kaḷai vaḻakkuraiñar tiru. ti. kōmati nāyakampiḷḷai avarkaḷ caripārttu utaviṉār.
kaḻakattiṉ mutaṟporuḷ urū. 50,000 eṉṟum, atu paṅku oṉṟukku urū. 10 viḻukkāṭu 5000 paṅkukaḷākak koṭukkappaṭum eṉṟum, tiruvaraṅkaṉārum tiraviyampiḷḷaiyum kūṭṭamaiccar
kaḷāka iruntu kaḻakattai naṭattuvatu eṉṟum, kaḻakam pativu ceyyap peṟṟa kālam mutal irupattaintu āṇṭukaḷ avarkaḷum avarkaḷ vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷum amaiccar
kaḷākavum kaḻakac ceyal kuḻu uṟuppiṉarkaḷākavum viḷaṅkik kaḻakattai naṭāttum urimai uṭaiyavar eṉṟum uṟutimuṟi eḻutappeṟṟatu. piṉ tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiya caivacittānta nūṟpatippukkaḻakam limiṭeṭ eṉum peyarāl 21-9-1920 il tirunelvēliyil kaḻakam pativu ceyyappeṟṟatu. tiru. va.cu. piṟanta cepṭampar 22ām nāḷ. kaḻakampativu ceyyap peṟṟa nāḷ cepṭampar 21 ām nāḷ. in nāḷnerukkam, ivarkkum kaḻakattiṟkum uḷḷa nerukkattaik kāṭṭum mutaṟkuṟippup pōlum.
kaḻakam pativāṉatum araṅkaṉār tirunelvēliyilēyē taṅkutaṟku nērntatu. ataṉālavar ceṉṉaiyil naṭattivanta tirucaṅkar kampeṉi puttaka vaṇikattait toṭarntu naṭattum poṟuppiṉai iḷavalāriṭam oppaṭaittār. 1921 pipravarit tiṅkaḷil tirucaṅkarkampeṉi irunta pavaḻakkāratteru iraṇṭām eṇ aṟaiyilēyē kaḻakak kiḷainilaiyam toṭaṅkappeṟṟatu. ataṉ mukavarāka va. cu. amarntār. atu mutal puttakavaṇikam kaḻakattiṉ peyarālalēyē naṭai
peṟalāyiṟṟu! tirucaṅkar kampeṉi eṉṉum ciṟṟāṟu, kaḻakap pērāṟṟuṭaṉ kalantu oṉṟāki amaintatu.
kaḻakattiṟkut tiraviyampiḷḷai vaḻiyāka vittiṭṭavar tiru. vicuvanātapiḷḷai allarō! avar pattu urūpā peṟumāṉa nūṟu paṅkukaḷai uvappuṭaṉ ēṟṟukkoṇṭār. tiṭṭappaṭi 5000 paṅkukaḷ allavō vēṇṭum? amaiccarkaḷ iruvarum paṅkukaḷ cērppatil muṉaintaṉar. tiraviyampiḷḷai pala tiṟappaṇikaḷil īṭupaṭṭavar. vāytta poḻutukiḷil maṭṭum kaḻakappaṇikaḷil karuttuk koḷvār. araṅkarō āviyum uṭalum uṭaimai ellāmum kaḻakattoṇṭukkē āḷākkip paṇiceytār. kaḻakam kālkoṇṭatu.
kaḻakam kāl koṇṭu kaviṉuṟat tulaṅkuvatai aṟintār tamiḻ nūl tokuppārvalar pākaṉēri mu. kācivicuvanātaṉ ceṭṭiyār. kaḻakat tiṭṭaṅkaḷum, kaṇṇaikkavarum ataṉ patippukaḷum avaraik kavarntaṉa. taṉavaiciya iḷaiñar tamiḻccaṅkat talaivarākavum, puravalarākavum, tikaḻum avar kaḻakap puravalarākavum amaiya ārvam koṇṭār. pattu urūpā peṟumāṉa nūṟu paṅkukaḷai avar ēṟṟukkoṇṭār. itaṉāl, kaḻakattoṭakkattil nūṟu paṅku eṭuttukkoṇṭa vicuvanātar kaḻakattiṟku vittiṭṭavar eṉṟāl, iv vicuvanātar kaḻakappayir ceḻittu naṟpayaṉ vaḻaṅka vēliyiṭṭa vittakarāvar eṉac cāṉṟōrkaḷāl pukaḻum pēṟu peṟṟār. iruvarum vicuvanātap pēruṭaiyār eṉpatu karutattakum ciṟappaṉṟō!


8. kaḻaka nōkkac ciṟappu
(taṉakkākavē piṟar vāḻavēṇṭum eṉpatu āṇavanōkku, tāṉē vāḻavēṇṭum eṉpatu mika mikak kuṟukiya nōkku, tāṉum vāḻavēṇṭum piṟarum vāḻavēṇṭum eṉpatu potu nōkku; taṉakkākap piṟarum, piṟarukkākat tāṉum vāḻa vēṇṭum eṉpatu oppunōkku; piṟarukkākavē tāṉvāḻa vēṇṭum eṉpatu perunōkku. in nōkkam uṭaiyavarāl tāṉ ulakam uṇkiṟatu.)
nōkku eṉpatu paḻantamiḻc col. ceyyuḷiyal kūṟavanta āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ataṉ uṟuppukaḷaik kūṟuṅkāl nōkku eṉpatoṉṟaiyum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. ataṉ ilakkaṇam iṉṉateṉavum teḷivikkiṟār. muḻutuṟa nōkkik kaḻi pēriṉpam koḷvatē nōkku eṉpatu avar karutum poruḷākum.
pārvai parantupaṭṭuc celvatu; kāṭci kaṇṇukkuk kaḷippūṭṭuvatu; nōkku uḷḷattiṟku uvakaiyūṭṭuvatu.
pārvai kaṇkaḷai iyalpāy akalat tiṟakkap piṟantu eṇum poruḷilum paravic cellum.
kāṭci kaṇṇai ārvattāl tiṟappittu uṇarvai uṇṭākkip pārāṭṭa vaikkum.
nōkkō, kuvintu ceṉṟu uḷḷattiṉ āḻattil patintu uṇarvai uṇṭākki meymmaṟakka vaikkum. in nōkkai nōkkutaṟ kāraṇam nōkkeṉap paṭumē eṉa nayamikak kuṟittār tolkāppiyaṉār. kavikaṇ nōkku eṉa nōkkiṉai ōviyamākkik kāṭṭiṉār oru caṅkac cāṉṟōr. nōkku, kātal poruṭṭātalai vaḷḷuvar mutalākac colliyal uṇarnta pulavarkaḷ ellārum cuvaikeḻumap pāṭiṉar.
nōkkil iruntu kiḷarntatu nōkkam. nōkkam ārvamikka kaṭaippiṭiyāyiṟṟu. atuvē kuṟikkōḷ āyiṟṟu. kōḷ eṉpatu eṉṉa? koḷḷukai, kaṭaippiṭi eṉka. māntar vāḻvu, nērkkoṭu paṭṭatu. illaiyeṉil, pōkkoṭu pōyk keṭṭoḻintatēyām. ittakaiya nōkkiṉ nuṭpamum, payaṉum, iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyum uṇarntu amaikkappaṭṭatē kaḻaka nōkkam ākum.
taṉiyāṭu mēyppatiṉum kūṭṭāṭu naṉṟām;
	iṉināṭē kūṭṭu ṟavai nāṭu
eṉpatu kūṭṭuṟaviṉ ciṟappu valiyuṟuttal. kūṭṭāṭu mēyttal nāṭṭup puṟaṅkaḷil kāṇakkūṭiyatē. kālvaluvāka ūṉṟavu māṭṭāta - ūṉṟutalai eṇṇavum māṭṭāta - kālattē, nūṟpatippu niṟuvaṉattil pukuttiyatē cīriya nōkkākum. in nōkkil tuvaṅkic ciṟanta paṇiceytuvarum potuk kaḻakam teṉṉāṭṭil iḵtu oṉṟēyākum.
kaḻaka ūtiyattil iruntu nūṟṟukku āṟu viḻukkāṭṭukku mēl paṅkāḷikaḷ ūtiyam peṟutaṟku urimai illai eṉpatu aṟantaḻuviya - poruḷē mutaṉmaiyeṉak karutāta - oru nōkkam ākum.
maṟṟoṉṟu, kaḻakattukku ēṟpaṭum celavu nīkkik kiṭaikkum ūtiyattil caripāti ik kaḻakattiṉ āṭciyil uḷḷa teṉṉintiya caivacittānta caṅkattiṟkum, teṉṉintiya tamiḻc caṅkattiṟkum koṭukkap peṟutal vēṇṭum eṉpatu. it tērcci mikka tiṭṭattāl ic caṅkaṅkaḷ caiva ulakukkum tamiḻ ulakukkum ceytuḷḷa - ceytuvarum - ciṟanta toṇṭukaḷ matippiṭaṟku ariyaṉa.
iṉṉum, entavoru veḷiyīṭṭakamum itukāṟum mēṟkoḷḷappeṟāta nikaraṟṟa iraṇṭu tiṭṭaṅkaḷai vakuttuk koṇṭatu kaḻakam.
oṉṟu : ārāycciyāḷarkku aruntuṇai yākattakka vakaiyil kiṭaittaṟku ariya nūlkaḷaiyellām tokuttu nūlnilaiyam ākkutal eṉpatu. it tiṭṭattāl ivvulakiṉ enta oru nūl nilaiyattilum tiṭṭavaṭṭamākap peṟutaṟku muṭiyavē muṭiyātu eṉattakka pallāyiram aruntamiḻ nūlkaḷai annāḷ mutalē tokuttu vaikkum pēṟu vāyttatu. iḵtu ulakameṅkum uḷḷa enta veḷiyīṭṭu niṟuvaṉamum ceytaṟiyāta ceyalākum.
maṟṟoṉṟu: itaḻ veḷiyiṭutal eṉṉum nōkkam. in nōkkattāl aruṅkalaic celvameṉa matittu aṭṭai kaṭṭi, vaittup pēḻaiyuḷ eṉṟum pōṟṟik kākkattakka karuvūlamākac centamiḻc celvi eṉṉum tiṅkaḷitaḻ taṭaippaṭutal illāmal 1923 ām āṇṭut toṭakka mutalē arumperun toṇṭāṟṟi varukiṟatu.
ivaiyellām kaḻaka nōkkac ciṟappiṉ aṭippaṭai
yāl nāṭu peṟṟa naṟpayaṉkaḷām.
iṉi, nūlkaḷilum ennūl āyiṉum veḷiyiṭṭu viṭuvatā? illai! avaṟṟaiyum muṉṉatākavē aṟutiyiṭṭu uṟutip paṭuttap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
caivacittānta nūlkaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṉ karuttukku māṟupāṭillāta paḻaiya aṭiṟavu nūlkaḷ, karuvi nūlkaḷ, ulaka nūlkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum ituvarai accukku varāta vaiyum, ippōtu kiṭaittaṟku ariyavāyuḷḷaṉavumāṉa nūlkaḷaiyum patippittu viṟpaṉai ceytal,
mēṟkuṟitta nūlkaḷukku aṟivuṭaiya periyār palaraik koṇṭu ārāyccikaḷ, viḷakkavuraikaḷ mutaliyaṉa eḻutuvittu avaṟṟaip patippittu viṟpaṉai ceytal.
vaṭamoḻiyilum maṟṟum piṟamoḻikaḷilumuḷḷa aṟiya nūlkaḷait tamiḻil moḻipeyarttup patippittal, viṟṟu mutal ceytal.
tamiḻ nūlkaḷai āṅkilattilum piṟamoḻikaḷilum moḻi peyarttup patippittu viṟṟumutal ceytal.
- ivaiyellām ettakaiya etirkāla nōkkoṭu amaikkap peṟṟavai eṉpataik karuti makiḻka.
iṉṉoṉṟu : tamiḻmoḻi innāḷil evvet tuṟai kaḷil peṟṟirukkum ēṟṟaṅkaḷukkum aṭippaṭaiyāka amainta kaḻakattiṉ tiṭṭamiṭṭa paṇikaḷēyām.. taṉittamiḻ iyakkap pācaṟaiyākat tikaḻntu varuvarum kaḻakamēyām. ivaṟṟuk kellām kāraṇarāka iruntavar evar? araṅkaṉārum, avar arumai iḷaval va. cu. vumē āvar aṉṟō!
kaḻakam eṉṉum col paḻaṅkālattil perumaip poruḷ taruvatāy illai. tivākaramuṉivar, parañcōti muṉivar ākiyōr kālattukkup piṉṉē tāṉ kaḻakam eṉpatu cāṉṟōr avaiyam eṉṉum poruṭciṟappuṭaiya collāyiṟṟu. ac collai nāṭaṟinta perumaic collāka māṟṟi vaḻaṅku vittatu ic caiva cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakamēyām. ik kaḻakam tōṉṟic cīriya paṇikaḷ ceytu kaḻakac collukkut taṉip perumai cērtta piṉṉarē, kaṭcikaḷum amaippukaḷum avaiyaṅkaḷum kaḻakap peyar koṇṭaṉa. ivaiyellām eṇṇi makiḻattakkavai eṉka.


9. āṭciyiṉ arumai
(ēr uḻutaliṉum nalamākum eruviṭutal; eru viṭutaliṉum nalamākum kaḷaiyeṭuttal; kaḷaiyeṭuttaliṉum nalamākum nīrppāyccutal; nīrppāyccutaliṉum nalamākum kāval purital; kāval cōrntālkaippiṭi tavacamēṉum kaḷañciyattiṟku vantu cērumō?)
āḷukai, āḷutal, āṭci eṉpaṉavellām oru poruḷ tarum coṟkaḷ. ovvōr āḷum - āṇ āḷ eṉiṉum peṇ āḷ eṉiṉum - āḷukait tiṟam vāyntavar eṉpatē it tamiḻc colliṉ kuṟippupporuḷākum. āṉāl, ip poruḷ viḷaṅka vāḻntu kāṭṭiyavar viral viṭṭu eṇṇat takkavarākavē uḷḷaṉar eṉpatu varalāṟu kāṭṭum uṇmai.
ōr āṭcipparappu, nāṭāka iruntāleṉṉa? vīṭāka iruntāl eṉṉa? oru niṟuvaṉamāka iruntāl eṉṉa? ōr amaippāka iruntāl eṉṉa? ataṉ ciṟappu ataṉai āḷpavar cīrmaiyaip poṟuttē amaikiṉṟatu.
maṉṉaṉ eppaṭi, makkaḷ appaṭi eṉpatu muṭiyāṭcik kālap paḻamoḻi. āḷpavar eppaṭi, vāḻpavar appaṭi eṉpatu kuṭiyāṭcikkālap putumoḻi. mēlnilaiyil iruntu āṇaiyiṭupavar cemmaiyilarāyiṉ avarkkuk kīḻnilaiyil iruntu paṇipurivāriṭam cemmai taṅkumō? cemmai taṅka villai eṉak kīḻnilaiyil iruppārai mēlnilaiyil iruppār taṭṭik kēṭkavum iyalumō?
itaṉaic caṅkaccāṉṟōr oruvar teḷḷitiṉ viḷakkiṉār. avar toṇṭaimāṉ iḷantiraiyaṉ eṉpār. āṭcippoṟuppil irunta avar āṭcipaṟṟik kūṟiya aṟivurai avar paṭṭaṟivālveḷippaṭṭatākaliṉ pōṟṟik koḷḷat takkatu eṉpatil aiyamiṉṟām.
uruḷaiyaiyum pāraiyum kōttu ulakiṉkaṇṇē celuttum kāvaluṭaiya vaṇṭi, ataṉaic celuttuvōṉ māṇpuṭaiyaṉ āyiṉ ūṟupāṭu illāmal vaḻiyil iṉitākac cellum. mt‹ mjid ïÅjhf¢ brY¤Jjiy¤ bjËa kh£lhjt‹ MÆ‹ mJ ehŸnjhW« gifah»¢, br¿ªj nr‰WŸ mGªâ Äf¥gy Ôa J‹g¤ij nkY« nkY« c©lh¡F«” v‹gJ mt® brhšÈa m¿îiuah«.*
kaḻaka vaṇṭiyaic celuttutalilum iṭarkaḷ mikappala uṇṭāyiṉa; etiriṭṭaṉa. eṉiṉum ataṉaic celuttuvōr tiṟam, ūṟupāṭukaḷai yellām aṟavē akaṟṟic cevviya cālaiyil cemmāntu cella utaviyatu.
kuḻantaikaḷukku eḷitil nōy paṟṟikkoḷḷal ulakaṟinta uṇmai. avvāṟē enta amaippukkum toṭakkattil tollaikaḷum tuyarkaḷum vantu muṭṭutal tavirkka muṭiyātavai. av vēḷaikaḷil ellām āṭcip poṟuppuṭaiyār tiṟaṅkaḷē amaippaik kaṭṭikkāttuḷḷaṉa. av vitikkuk kaḻakamum urimai pūṇṭatēyām.
1920 ām āṇṭil kaḻakam toṭaṅkiya nāḷmutal āṭcip poṟuppil iruntu arumpāṭupaṭṭavar tiruvaraṅkaṉār. akkālattil kaḻakappaṅkukaḷ cērkka avar eṭuttukkoṇṭa muyaṟcikku aḷavē illai. perumpālāṉa paṅkukaḷait tiraṭṭik kaḻakattai nilaipeṟuttiya kaḻakak kāvalar avarē eṉil mikaiyaṉṟām.
akkālattil iḷavalār va. cu. ceṉṉaik kiḷaiyiṉ mukavarāka iruntār. avar nūl veḷiyīṭṭukkākavum avaṟṟai viṟpataṟkākavum eṭuttukkoṇṭa muyaṟcikaḷ aḷavil aṭaṅkātaṉa. ivviruvakai muyaṟcikaḷum irupālum iṉitiṉ iyaṅkiyamaiyāltāṉ kaḻakam taṉ kālai valimaiyāka ūṉṟi nilaipeṟalāyiṟṟu.
